## 🔵 ANALYTICS SCHEMA — VIEWS

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- ANALYTICS: KEY REPORTING VIEWS
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Daily Hospital Census
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_daily_census AS
SELECT
    a.branch_id,
    b.name AS branch_name,
    DATE(NOW()) AS census_date,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' THEN 1 END) AS total_inpatients,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'icu' THEN 1 END) AS icu_patients,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'nicu' THEN 1 END) AS nicu_patients,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.admission_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END) AS new_admissions_today,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN d.discharge_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END) AS discharges_today,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'maternity' THEN 1 END) AS maternity_patients
FROM clinical.admissions a
JOIN platform.branches b ON a.branch_id = b.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON a.ward_id = w.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON a.id = d.admission_id
GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name;

-- 2. Bed Occupancy Rate
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_bed_occupancy AS
SELECT
    w.branch_id,
    br.name AS branch_name,
    w.id AS ward_id,
    w.name AS ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    COUNT(b.id) AS total_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END) AS occupied_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'available' THEN 1 END) AS available_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'cleaning' THEN 1 END) AS cleaning_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'maintenance' THEN 1 END) AS maintenance_beds,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
        NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 2
    ) AS occupancy_rate_pct
FROM facility.wards w
JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN facility.beds b ON b.ward_id = w.id AND b.is_active = TRUE
WHERE w.is_active = TRUE
GROUP BY w.branch_id, br.name, w.id, w.name, w.ward_type;

-- 3. Financial Summary View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_financial_summary AS
SELECT
    b.branch_id,
    br.name AS branch_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', b.bill_date) AS month,
    COUNT(b.id) AS total_bills,
    SUM(b.gross_amount) AS total_gross,
    SUM(b.discount_amount) AS total_discount,
    SUM(b.net_amount) AS total_net,
    SUM(b.paid_amount) AS total_collected,
    SUM(b.insurance_amount) AS total_insurance,
    SUM(b.balance_amount) AS total_outstanding,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'pharmacy' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END) AS pharmacy_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'opd' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END) AS opd_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'ipd' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END) AS ipd_revenue
FROM finance.bills b
JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
WHERE b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
GROUP BY b.branch_id, br.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', b.bill_date);

-- 4. Doctor Performance View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_doctor_performance AS
SELECT
    d.id AS doctor_id,
    e.full_name AS doctor_name,
    e.department_id,
    dep.name AS department,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', v.visit_date) AS month,
    COUNT(v.id) AS total_opd_patients,
    COUNT(s.id) AS total_surgeries,
    COUNT(a.id) AS total_admissions,
    ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (v.visit_date - v.visit_date)))/60, 0) AS avg_consult_min,
    SUM(bi.net_amount) AS revenue_generated
FROM hr.doctors d
JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.patient_visits v ON v.visit_id IS NOT NULL
LEFT JOIN clinical.surgeries s ON s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
LEFT JOIN finance.bill_items bi ON bi.item_ref_id = d.id
GROUP BY d.id, e.full_name, e.department_id, dep.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', v.visit_date);

-- 5. Lab TAT Performance View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_lab_tat_performance AS
SELECT
    lo.branch_id,
    tm.test_name,
    tc.name AS category,
    COUNT(lr.id) AS total_tests,
    ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600)::NUMERIC, 2) AS avg_tat_hours,
    tm.tat_hours AS target_tat_hours,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600 > tm.tat_hours THEN 1 END) AS breached_count,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600 > tm.tat_hours THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL 
        / NULLIF(COUNT(lr.id), 0) * 100, 2
    ) AS breach_rate_pct
FROM clinical.lab_results lr
JOIN clinical.lab_order_items loi ON lr.order_item_id = loi.id
JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON loi.order_id = lo.id
JOIN clinical.lab_samples ls ON lr.sample_id = ls.id
JOIN clinical.test_master tm ON lr.test_id = tm.id
JOIN clinical.test_categories tc ON tm.category_id = tc.id
WHERE ls.collected_at IS NOT NULL AND lr.entered_at IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY lo.branch_id, tm.test_name, tc.name, tm.tat_hours;

-- 6. Pharmacy Stock Status View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_pharmacy_stock_status AS
SELECT
    ms.branch_id,
    br.name AS branch_name,
    m.generic_name,
    m.brand_name,
    m.form,
    SUM(ms.quantity) AS total_quantity,
    MIN(ms.expiry_date) AS nearest_expiry,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days' THEN 1 END) AS expiring_30_days,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END) AS expired_count,
    m.is_controlled,
    m.is_narcotic
FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON ms.branch_id = br.id
WHERE ms.status = 'active'
GROUP BY ms.branch_id, br.name, m.id, m.generic_name, m.brand_name, m.form, m.is_controlled, m.is_narcotic;

-- 7. Insurance Claims Aging View  
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_insurance_claims_aging AS
SELECT
    ic.org_id,
    ins.name AS insurance_company,
    COUNT(ic.id) AS total_claims,
    SUM(ic.claimed_amount) AS total_claimed,
    SUM(ic.approved_amount) AS total_approved,
    SUM(ic.settled_amount) AS total_settled,
    SUM(ic.claimed_amount - COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0)) AS outstanding,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status = 'submitted' AND ic.submitted_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days' THEN 1 END) AS aging_30_60,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status = 'submitted' AND ic.submitted_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '60 days' THEN 1 END) AS aging_60_90,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status = 'submitted' AND ic.submitted_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '90 days' THEN 1 END) AS aging_90_plus
FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
JOIN finance.insurance_companies ins ON ic.policy_id IS NOT NULL
WHERE ic.status NOT IN ('settled', 'rejected')
GROUP BY ic.org_id, ins.name;
```

---

## 🔵 STORED PROCEDURES — REPORT GENERATORS

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SP 1: DAILY HOSPITAL CENSUS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_daily_census_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_date      DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    metric          TEXT,
    value           BIGINT,
    change_vs_prev  BIGINT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_prev_date DATE := p_date - INTERVAL '1 day';
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH today AS (
        SELECT
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted') AS inpatients,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.admission_datetime::DATE = p_date) AS new_admissions,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE d.discharge_datetime::DATE = p_date) AS discharges,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'icu') AS icu,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'nicu') AS nicu,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'maternity') AS maternity
        FROM clinical.admissions a
        JOIN facility.wards w ON a.ward_id = w.id
        LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
        WHERE a.branch_id = p_branch_id
    ),
    prev AS (
        SELECT
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted') AS inpatients,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.admission_datetime::DATE = v_prev_date) AS new_admissions,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE d.discharge_datetime::DATE = v_prev_date) AS discharges
        FROM clinical.admissions a
        LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
        WHERE a.branch_id = p_branch_id
    )
    SELECT 'Total Inpatients'::TEXT,    today.inpatients,     today.inpatients - prev.inpatients FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'New Admissions'::TEXT,      today.new_admissions, today.new_admissions - prev.new_admissions FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Discharges'::TEXT,          today.discharges,     today.discharges - prev.discharges FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'ICU Patients'::TEXT,        today.icu,            0 FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'NICU Patients'::TEXT,       today.nicu,           0 FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Maternity Patients'::TEXT,  today.maternity,      0 FROM today, prev;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 2: REVENUE REPORT (By Period & Department)
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_revenue_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_branch_id     UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_group_by      TEXT DEFAULT 'day'   -- day/week/month
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    period          TEXT,
    branch_name     TEXT,
    bill_type       TEXT,
    total_bills     BIGINT,
    gross_amount    NUMERIC,
    discount        NUMERIC,
    net_revenue     NUMERIC,
    collected       NUMERIC,
    outstanding     NUMERIC,
    collection_rate NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        CASE p_group_by
            WHEN 'month' THEN TO_CHAR(b.bill_date, 'YYYY-MM')
            WHEN 'week'  THEN TO_CHAR(DATE_TRUNC('week', b.bill_date), 'YYYY-WW')
            ELSE              TO_CHAR(b.bill_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD')
        END                                     AS period,
        br.name                                 AS branch_name,
        COALESCE(b.bill_type, 'All')           AS bill_type,
        COUNT(b.id)::BIGINT                    AS total_bills,
        ROUND(SUM(b.gross_amount)::NUMERIC, 2) AS gross_amount,
        ROUND(SUM(b.discount_amount)::NUMERIC, 2) AS discount,
        ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)   AS net_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)  AS collected,
        ROUND(SUM(b.balance_amount)::NUMERIC, 2) AS outstanding,
        ROUND(
            SUM(b.paid_amount) / NULLIF(SUM(b.net_amount), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC                             AS collection_rate
    FROM finance.bills b
    JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE b.org_id = p_org_id
      AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR b.branch_id = p_branch_id)
      AND b.bill_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
    GROUP BY period, br.name, b.bill_type
    ORDER BY period DESC, br.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 3: BED OCCUPANCY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_bed_occupancy_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    ward_name           TEXT,
    ward_type           TEXT,
    total_beds          BIGINT,
    avg_occupied        NUMERIC,
    occupancy_rate_pct  NUMERIC,
    avg_los_days        NUMERIC,
    total_admissions    BIGINT,
    total_discharges    BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        w.name                                      AS ward_name,
        w.ward_type,
        COUNT(DISTINCT b.id)::BIGINT               AS total_beds,
        ROUND(AVG(
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.admissions a2
             WHERE a2.ward_id = w.id AND a2.status = 'admitted')
        )::NUMERIC, 1)                             AS avg_occupied,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN b.id END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT b.id), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC                                 AS occupancy_rate_pct,
        ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1)  AS avg_los_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN a.admission_datetime::DATE 
                    BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS total_admissions,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN d.discharge_datetime::DATE 
                    BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS total_discharges
    FROM facility.wards w
    JOIN facility.beds b ON b.ward_id = w.id AND b.is_active = TRUE
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.ward_id = w.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
    WHERE w.branch_id = p_branch_id AND w.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY w.name, w.ward_type
    ORDER BY occupancy_rate_pct DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 4: DOCTOR PERFORMANCE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_doctor_performance_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_department_id UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    doctor_name         TEXT,
    specialization      TEXT,
    department_name     TEXT,
    opd_patients        BIGINT,
    ipd_admissions      BIGINT,
    surgeries           BIGINT,
    revenue_generated   NUMERIC,
    avg_rating          NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        e.full_name                             AS doctor_name,
        d.specialization,
        dep.name                                AS department_name,
        COUNT(DISTINCT v.id)::BIGINT           AS opd_patients,
        COUNT(DISTINCT a.id)::BIGINT           AS ipd_admissions,
        COUNT(DISTINCT s.id)::BIGINT           AS surgeries,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS revenue_generated,
        ROUND(AVG(4.2)::NUMERIC, 1)            AS avg_rating  -- placeholder for rating
    FROM hr.doctors d
    JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.patient_visits v 
        ON v.visit_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a 
        ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
        AND a.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN clinical.surgeries s 
        ON s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id
        AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN finance.bill_items bi ON bi.item_ref_id = d.id
    WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id
      AND (p_department_id IS NULL OR e.department_id = p_department_id)
    GROUP BY e.full_name, d.specialization, dep.name
    ORDER BY revenue_generated DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 5: PHARMACY STOCK EXPIRY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_expiry_report(
    p_branch_id     UUID,
    p_days_ahead    INTEGER DEFAULT 90
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    medicine_name   TEXT,
    brand_name      TEXT,
    batch_no        TEXT,
    expiry_date     DATE,
    days_to_expiry  INTEGER,
    quantity        INTEGER,
    expiry_status   TEXT,
    value           NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        m.generic_name          AS medicine_name,
        m.brand_name,
        ms.batch_no,
        ms.expiry_date,
        (ms.expiry_date - CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER AS days_to_expiry,
        ms.quantity,
        CASE
            WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE                      THEN '🔴 EXPIRED'
            WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days' THEN '🟠 < 30 Days'
            WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '60 days' THEN '🟡 < 60 Days'
            ELSE                                                         '🟢 < 90 Days'
        END                     AS expiry_status,
        ROUND((ms.quantity * ms.purchase_price)::NUMERIC, 2) AS value
    FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
    JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
    WHERE ms.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND ms.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + (p_days_ahead || ' days')::INTERVAL
      AND ms.quantity > 0
      AND ms.status = 'active'
    ORDER BY ms.expiry_date ASC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 6: INSURANCE CLAIM STATUS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_insurance_claim_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_status_filter TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    claim_no            TEXT,
    patient_name        TEXT,
    insurance_company   TEXT,
    admission_date      DATE,
    claimed_amount      NUMERIC,
    approved_amount     NUMERIC,
    settled_amount      NUMERIC,
    outstanding         NUMERIC,
    claim_status        TEXT,
    days_pending        INTEGER
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        ic.claim_no,
        p.full_name                             AS patient_name,
        ins.name                                AS insurance_company,
        a.admission_datetime::DATE              AS admission_date,
        ROUND(ic.claimed_amount::NUMERIC, 2)    AS claimed_amount,
        ROUND(COALESCE(ic.approved_amount, 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS approved_amount,
        ROUND(COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0)::NUMERIC, 2)  AS settled_amount,
        ROUND((ic.claimed_amount - COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS outstanding,
        ic.status                               AS claim_status,
        (CURRENT_DATE - ic.submitted_at::DATE)::INTEGER AS days_pending
    FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
    JOIN clinical.patients p ON ic.patient_id = p.id
    JOIN finance.patient_insurance_policies pip ON ic.policy_id = pip.id
    JOIN finance.insurance_companies ins ON pip.company_id = ins.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON ic.admission_id = a.id
    WHERE ic.org_id = p_org_id
      AND ic.claim_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND (p_status_filter IS NULL OR ic.status = p_status_filter)
    ORDER BY ic.claim_date DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 7: PATIENT DISCHARGE SUMMARY
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_discharge_summary(
    p_admission_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    field   TEXT,
    value   TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT * FROM (
        SELECT 1 AS sort, 'Patient Name'::TEXT,    p.full_name::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a
        JOIN clinical.patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id
        WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 2, 'MRN',            p.mrn::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a JOIN clinical.patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 3, 'Admission No',   a.admission_no::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 4, 'Admission Date', a.admission_datetime::DATE::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 5, 'Discharge Date', d.discharge_datetime::DATE::TEXT
        FROM clinical.discharges d WHERE d.admission_id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 6, 'Length of Stay', a.length_of_stay::TEXT || ' days'
        FROM clinical.admissions a WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 7, 'Discharge Type', d.discharge_type::TEXT
        FROM clinical.discharges d WHERE d.admission_id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 8, 'Final Diagnosis', d.final_diagnosis::TEXT
        FROM clinical.discharges d WHERE d.admission_id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 9, 'Total Bill', ROUND(b.net_amount, 2)::TEXT
        FROM finance.bills b WHERE b.admission_id = p_admission_id AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled')
        ORDER BY sort
    ) sub(sort, field, value);
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 8: PAYROLL PROCESSING
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION hr.sp_process_payroll(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_month     INTEGER,
    p_year      INTEGER,
    p_processed_by UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    employee_code   TEXT,
    employee_name   TEXT,
    present_days    NUMERIC,
    gross_salary    NUMERIC,
    deductions      NUMERIC,
    net_salary      NUMERIC,
    status          TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_run_id UUID;
    v_working_days INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Calculate working days in month
    v_working_days := 26; -- configurable
    
    -- Create payroll run
    INSERT INTO hr.payroll_runs(
        org_id, branch_id, pay_month, pay_year, status, processed_at
    )
    VALUES (p_org_id, p_branch_id, p_month, p_year, 'processing', NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (org_id, branch_id, pay_month, pay_year)
    DO UPDATE SET status = 'processing', processed_at = NOW()
    RETURNING id INTO v_run_id;
    
    -- Process each employee
    INSERT INTO hr.payslips (
        payroll_run_id, employee_id, pay_month, pay_year,
        working_days, present_days, basic_salary,
        gross_salary, total_deduction, net_salary, status
    )
    SELECT
        v_run_id,
        e.id,
        p_month,
        p_year,
        v_working_days,
        COUNT(att.id),
        e.basic_salary,
        e.basic_salary * 1.5,                   -- simplified: basic + allowances
        e.basic_salary * 0.15,                  -- simplified: tax + PF
        e.basic_salary * 1.5 - e.basic_salary * 0.15,
        'draft'
    FROM hr.employees e
    LEFT JOIN hr.attendance att ON att.employee_id = e.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM att.attendance_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM att.attendance_date) = p_year
        AND att.status = 'present'
    WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id
      AND e.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND e.status = 'active'
    GROUP BY e.id, e.basic_salary
    ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
    
    -- Update run status
    UPDATE hr.payroll_runs
    SET status = 'approved',
        total_gross = (SELECT SUM(gross_salary) FROM hr.payslips WHERE payroll_run_id = v_run_id),
        total_deduction = (SELECT SUM(total_deduction) FROM hr.payslips WHERE payroll_run_id = v_run_id),
        total_net = (SELECT SUM(net_salary) FROM hr.payslips WHERE payroll_run_id = v_run_id)
    WHERE id = v_run_id;
    
    -- Return result
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        e.employee_code,
        e.full_name,
        ps.present_days,
        ROUND(ps.gross_salary::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(ps.total_deduction::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(ps.net_salary::NUMERIC, 2),
        ps.status
    FROM hr.payslips ps
    JOIN hr.employees e ON ps.employee_id = e.id
    WHERE ps.payroll_run_id = v_run_id
    ORDER BY e.full_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 9: OT UTILIZATION REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_ot_utilization_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    ot_room_name        TEXT,
    ot_type             TEXT,
    total_surgeries     BIGINT,
    completed           BIGINT,
    cancelled           BIGINT,
    avg_duration_min    NUMERIC,
    total_ot_hours      NUMERIC,
    cancellation_rate   NUMERIC,
    top_surgeon         TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        ot.name                                 AS ot_room_name,
        ot.ot_type,
        COUNT(s.id)::BIGINT                    AS total_surgeries,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN s.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS completed,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN s.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS cancelled,
        ROUND(AVG(s.duration_minutes)::NUMERIC, 0) AS avg_duration_min,
        ROUND(SUM(s.duration_minutes)::NUMERIC / 60, 1) AS total_ot_hours,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN s.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(s.id), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC AS cancellation_rate,
        (SELECT e2.full_name FROM hr.doctors d2
         JOIN hr.employees e2 ON d2.employee_id = e2.id
         WHERE d2.id = MODE() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY s.primary_surgeon_id)
         LIMIT 1) AS top_surgeon
    FROM clinical.ot_rooms ot
    LEFT JOIN clinical.surgeries s ON s.ot_room_id = ot.id
        AND s.scheduled_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    WHERE ot.branch_id = p_branch_id AND ot.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY ot.name, ot.ot_type
    ORDER BY total_ot_hours DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 10: EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD KPIs
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_executive_dashboard(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_date      DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    kpi_category    TEXT,
    kpi_name        TEXT,
    current_value   NUMERIC,
    prev_month_value NUMERIC,
    target_value    NUMERIC,
    unit            TEXT,
    status          TEXT     -- green/yellow/red
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    
    -- Clinical KPIs
    SELECT 'Clinical'::TEXT, 'Bed Occupancy Rate'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
                NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 1)
         FROM facility.beds b
         JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
         JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND b.is_active = TRUE),
        85.0::NUMERIC,   -- prev month placeholder
        85.0::NUMERIC,   -- target
        '%'::TEXT,
        'green'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Clinical'::TEXT, 'Average Length of Stay'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1)
         FROM clinical.admissions a
         JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
           AND a.admission_datetime >= DATE_TRUNC('month', p_date)),
        4.2::NUMERIC, 4.0::NUMERIC, 'Days'::TEXT, 'green'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Financial'::TEXT, 'Monthly Revenue'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 0)
         FROM finance.bills b
         WHERE b.org_id = p_org_id
           AND b.bill_date >= DATE_TRUNC('month', p_date)
           AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')),
        NULL::NUMERIC, NULL::NUMERIC, 'BDT'::TEXT, 'green'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Financial'::TEXT, 'Collection Rate'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount) / NULLIF(SUM(b.net_amount), 0) * 100, 1)::NUMERIC
         FROM finance.bills b
         WHERE b.org_id = p_org_id
           AND b.bill_date >= DATE_TRUNC('month', p_date)
           AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')),
        NULL::NUMERIC, 90.0::NUMERIC, '%'::TEXT, 'yellow'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Pharmacy'::TEXT, 'Expired Medicine Count'::TEXT,
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC
         FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
         JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
         JOIN platform.branches br ON ms.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE AND ms.quantity > 0),
        NULL::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC, 'Batches'::TEXT, 'red'::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

-- ============================================================
-- PERFORMANCE INDEXES
-- ============================================================

-- Patients
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_org       ON clinical.patients(org_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_mrn       ON clinical.patients(mrn);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_phone     ON clinical.patients(phone);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_nid       ON clinical.patients(nid_no);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_name      ON clinical.patients USING GIN(
    to_tsvector('simple', COALESCE(full_name, '') || ' ' || COALESCE(full_name_bn, ''))
);

-- Admissions
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_patient ON clinical.admissions(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_branch  ON clinical.admissions(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_status  ON clinical.admissions(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_date    ON clinical.admissions(admission_datetime);

-- Bills
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_patient      ON finance.bills(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_branch       ON finance.bills(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_date         ON finance.bills(bill_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_status       ON finance.bills(status);

-- Lab Results
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_patient ON clinical.lab_results(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_critical ON clinical.lab_results(is_critical) WHERE is_critical = TRUE;

-- Pharmacy Stock
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_branch       ON pharmacy.medicine_stock(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_expiry       ON pharmacy.medicine_stock(expiry_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_medicine     ON pharmacy.medicine_stock(medicine_id);

-- Audit Logs (Partitioned)
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_user         ON audit.audit_logs(user_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_table        ON audit.audit_logs(table_name, created_at);

-- Attendance
CREATE INDEX idx_attendance_emp     ON hr.attendance(employee_id, attendance_date);

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- FACILITY: MAINTENANCE MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE facility.equipment_registry (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    equipment_name      VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    equipment_code      VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE,
    category            VARCHAR(50),    -- medical/non_medical/it/hvac/electrical
    model_no            VARCHAR(100),
    serial_no           VARCHAR(100),
    manufacturer        VARCHAR(200),
    vendor_id           UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    purchase_date       DATE,
    purchase_price      DECIMAL(14,2),
    warranty_expiry     DATE,
    amc_start_date      DATE,
    amc_end_date        DATE,
    amc_vendor_id       UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    amc_amount          DECIMAL(12,2),
    location_ward_id    UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    location_room       VARCHAR(100),
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active', -- active/under_maintenance/condemned
    manual_url          TEXT,
    photo_url           TEXT,
    last_calibration    DATE,
    next_calibration    DATE,
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE facility.maintenance_schedules (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    equipment_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    schedule_type       VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- daily/weekly/monthly/quarterly/annual
    task_description    TEXT NOT NULL,
    checklist           JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    assigned_to         UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    vendor_id           UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    frequency_days      INTEGER,
    last_done_date      DATE,
    next_due_date       DATE,
    estimated_hours     DECIMAL(5,2),
    estimated_cost      DECIMAL(12,2),
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE facility.maintenance_work_orders (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    equipment_id        UUID REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    schedule_id         UUID REFERENCES facility.maintenance_schedules(id),
    wo_no               VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    wo_type             VARCHAR(30),    -- preventive/breakdown/calibration/civil/electrical
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal', -- critical/high/normal/low
    title               VARCHAR(300),
    description         TEXT,
    reported_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    reported_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    assigned_to         UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    vendor_id           UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    
    -- Timing
    scheduled_date      DATE,
    started_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    downtime_hours      DECIMAL(8,2),
    
    -- Resolution
    root_cause          TEXT,
    action_taken        TEXT,
    parts_used          JSONB DEFAULT '[]',  -- [{name, qty, cost}]
    labor_cost          DECIMAL(12,2),
    parts_cost          DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_cost          DECIMAL(12,2),
    
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'open', -- open/assigned/in_progress/completed/cancelled
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-compute total cost trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.compute_wo_total_cost()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.total_cost := COALESCE(NEW.labor_cost, 0) + COALESCE(NEW.parts_cost, 0);
    IF NEW.completed_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.started_at IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.downtime_hours := ROUND(
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NEW.completed_at - NEW.started_at)) / 3600, 2
        );
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_wo_total_cost
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON facility.maintenance_work_orders
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.compute_wo_total_cost();

CREATE TABLE facility.calibration_records (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    equipment_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    calibration_date    DATE NOT NULL,
    next_due_date       DATE,
    performed_by        VARCHAR(200),   -- internal/external agency
    agency_name         VARCHAR(200),
    certificate_no      VARCHAR(100),
    certificate_url     TEXT,
    result              VARCHAR(30),    -- pass/fail/conditional
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-alert for calibration due
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.alert_calibration_due()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.next_due_date IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE facility.equipment_registry
        SET next_calibration = NEW.next_due_date,
            last_calibration = NEW.calibration_date
        WHERE id = NEW.equipment_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_calibration_update
AFTER INSERT ON facility.calibration_records
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.alert_calibration_due();

CREATE TABLE facility.spare_parts_inventory (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    part_name       VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    part_code       VARCHAR(50),
    equipment_id    UUID REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    quantity        INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    min_quantity    INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    unit_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),
    supplier_id     UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    location        VARCHAR(100),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- FACILITY: BUILDING MANAGEMENT (Extended)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE facility.utility_meters (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    meter_type      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- electricity/water/gas/generator
    meter_no        VARCHAR(100),
    location        VARCHAR(200),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE facility.utility_readings (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    meter_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.utility_meters(id),
    reading_date    DATE NOT NULL,
    reading_value   DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
    unit            VARCHAR(20),    -- kWh/liter/cubic_meter
    consumption     DECIMAL(12,2),  -- computed from prev reading
    unit_rate       DECIMAL(10,4),
    total_cost      DECIMAL(12,2),
    read_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-compute consumption trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.compute_utility_consumption()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_prev_reading DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    SELECT reading_value INTO v_prev_reading
    FROM facility.utility_readings
    WHERE meter_id = NEW.meter_id AND reading_date < NEW.reading_date
    ORDER BY reading_date DESC
    LIMIT 1;

    IF v_prev_reading IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.consumption := NEW.reading_value - v_prev_reading;
        NEW.total_cost  := NEW.consumption * COALESCE(NEW.unit_rate, 0);
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_utility_consumption
BEFORE INSERT ON facility.utility_readings
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.compute_utility_consumption();

CREATE TABLE facility.security_incidents (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    incident_type   VARCHAR(50),    -- theft/fire/violence/accident/intrusion
    incident_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    location        VARCHAR(200),
    description     TEXT,
    reported_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    assigned_to     UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'open',
    resolution      TEXT,
    resolved_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

-- ============================================================

    org_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),

    item_code           VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,

    min_stock           INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    max_stock           INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    standard_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),

);

    floor_id        UUID REFERENCES facility.floors(id),

-- ============================================================
-- PROCUREMENT CYCLE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_requisitions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    pr_no               VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    requested_by        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    department_id       UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id),
    required_date       DATE,
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft/submitted/approved/po_raised/rejected
    approved_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    rejection_reason    TEXT,

);

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_requisition_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),

    unit            VARCHAR(30),

);

CREATE TABLE operations.request_for_quotations (

CREATE TABLE operations.rfq_suppliers (

CREATE TABLE operations.quotations (

    remarks         TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.quotation_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    quotation_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.quotations(id),

);

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_orders (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    pr_id               UUID REFERENCES operations.purchase_requisitions(id),
    quotation_id        UUID REFERENCES operations.quotations(id),
    po_no               VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    po_date             DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    supplier_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    delivery_date       DATE,
    payment_terms       VARCHAR(100),
    delivery_address    TEXT,
    gross_amount        DECIMAL(14,2),
    discount_amount     DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount          DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount          DECIMAL(14,2),
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft/approved/sent/partial/received/closed/cancelled
    approved_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_order_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    po_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.purchase_orders(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    ordered_qty     DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    received_qty    DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    pending_qty     DECIMAL(10,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (ordered_qty - received_qty) STORED,
    unit_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    discount_pct    DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_pct         DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_price       DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_amount    DECIMAL(14,2),
    remarks         TEXT
);

-- ============================================================
-- GOODS RECEIPT & STOCK
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.goods_receipt_notes (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    po_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.purchase_orders(id),
    store_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.stores(id),
    grn_no          VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    grn_date        DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    supplier_id     UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    invoice_no      VARCHAR(100),
    invoice_date    DATE,
    invoice_amount  DECIMAL(14,2),
    qc_status       VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending/passed/failed/partial
    qc_by           UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    received_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft/posted/cancelled
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.grn_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    grn_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.goods_receipt_notes(id),
    po_item_id      UUID REFERENCES operations.purchase_order_items(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    ordered_qty     DECIMAL(10,2),
    received_qty    DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    accepted_qty    DECIMAL(10,2),
    rejected_qty    DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    rejection_reason VARCHAR(200),
    batch_no        VARCHAR(100),
    expiry_date     DATE,
    unit_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_price     DECIMAL(14,2)
);

CREATE TABLE operations.stock_ledger (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    store_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.stores(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    transaction_type VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- grn/issue/return/transfer_in/transfer_out/adjustment/opening
    reference_type  VARCHAR(50),           -- grn/indent/transfer/adjustment
    reference_id    UUID,
    batch_no        VARCHAR(100),
    expiry_date     DATE,
    transaction_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    in_qty          DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    out_qty         DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    balance_qty     DECIMAL(10,2),
    unit_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_value     DECIMAL(14,2),
    narration       TEXT,
    created_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Current stock balance materialized view
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW operations.mv_current_stock AS
SELECT
    sl.store_id,
    st.name AS store_name,
    st.branch_id,
    sl.item_id,
    im.item_name,
    im.item_code,
    im.unit_of_measure,
    SUM(sl.in_qty - sl.out_qty) AS current_qty,
    SUM(sl.total_value) AS stock_value,
    im.reorder_level,
    im.min_stock,
    CASE
        WHEN SUM(sl.in_qty - sl.out_qty) <= im.min_stock    THEN 'critical'
        WHEN SUM(sl.in_qty - sl.out_qty) <= im.reorder_level THEN 'low'
        ELSE 'normal'
    END AS stock_status
FROM operations.stock_ledger sl
JOIN operations.stores st ON sl.store_id = st.id
JOIN operations.item_master im ON sl.item_id = im.id
GROUP BY sl.store_id, st.name, st.branch_id, sl.item_id, im.item_name, im.item_code,
         im.unit_of_measure, im.reorder_level, im.min_stock;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON operations.mv_current_stock(store_id, item_id);

-- Refresh stock view on ledger insert
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.refresh_stock_view()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY operations.mv_current_stock;
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_refresh_stock
AFTER INSERT ON operations.stock_ledger
FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.refresh_stock_view();

-- ============================================================
-- DEPARTMENT INDENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.department_indents (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    department_id   UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id),
    from_store_id   UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.stores(id),
    indent_no       VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    indent_date     DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    required_date   DATE,
    requested_by    UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    issued_by       UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending/approved/issued/rejected
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.indent_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    indent_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.department_indents(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    requested_qty   DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    approved_qty    DECIMAL(10,2),
    issued_qty      DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    unit_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),
    remarks         TEXT
);

-- Post stock ledger on indent issue
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.post_indent_to_ledger()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'issued' AND OLD.status != 'issued' THEN
        INSERT INTO operations.stock_ledger(
            store_id, item_id, transaction_type, reference_type,
            reference_id, transaction_date, out_qty, narration, created_at
        )
        SELECT
            NEW.from_store_id,
            ii.item_id,
            'issue',
            'indent',
            NEW.id,
            CURRENT_DATE,
            COALESCE(ii.issued_qty, 0),
            'Issue against Indent: ' || NEW.indent_no,
            NOW()
        FROM operations.indent_items ii
        WHERE ii.indent_id = NEW.id AND COALESCE(ii.issued_qty, 0) > 0;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_indent_post_ledger
AFTER UPDATE ON operations.department_indents
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.post_indent_to_ledger();

-- Post stock ledger on GRN
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.post_grn_to_ledger()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'posted' AND OLD.status != 'posted' THEN
        INSERT INTO operations.stock_ledger(
            store_id, item_id, transaction_type, reference_type,
            reference_id, batch_no, expiry_date, transaction_date,
            in_qty, unit_cost, total_value, narration, created_at
        )
        SELECT
            NEW.store_id,
            gi.item_id,
            'grn',
            'grn',
            NEW.id,
            gi.batch_no,
            gi.expiry_date,
            NEW.grn_date,
            COALESCE(gi.accepted_qty, gi.received_qty),
            gi.unit_price,
            COALESCE(gi.accepted_qty, gi.received_qty) * COALESCE(gi.unit_price, 0),
            'GRN: ' || NEW.grn_no,
            NOW()
        FROM operations.grn_items gi
        WHERE gi.grn_id = NEW.id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_grn_post_ledger
AFTER UPDATE ON operations.goods_receipt_notes
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.post_grn_to_ledger();
```

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: CONSENT FORM MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.consent_templates (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    consent_type    VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                    -- general/surgical/anesthesia/blood_transfusion/procedure/
                    -- telemedicine/research/photography/data_sharing/dnar
    title           VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    title_bn        VARCHAR(300),
    content_en      TEXT NOT NULL,
    content_bn      TEXT,
    version         VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT '1.0',
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_consents (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    surgery_id          UUID REFERENCES clinical.surgeries(id),
    template_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.consent_templates(id),
    consent_type        VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Consent process
    explained_by        UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),   -- doctor
    explained_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    language_used       VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'bn',
    interpreter_used    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    interpreter_name    VARCHAR(200),
    
    -- Consent given
    consented_by        VARCHAR(200),                         -- patient/guardian name
    relation_to_patient VARCHAR(50),                          -- self/guardian/spouse
    signed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    patient_signature   TEXT,                                 -- base64 / URL
    witness_signature   TEXT,
    witness_name        VARCHAR(200),
    
    -- Status
    is_withdrawn        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    withdrawn_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    withdrawn_by        UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    withdrawal_reason   TEXT,
    
    -- Storage
    pdf_url             TEXT,
    is_verified         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Alert if consent missing before surgery
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.check_consent_before_surgery()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_consent_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'in_progress' THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_consent_count
        FROM clinical.patient_consents
        WHERE surgery_id = NEW.id
          AND consent_type IN ('surgical', 'anesthesia')
          AND is_withdrawn = FALSE;

        IF v_consent_count < 2 THEN
            RAISE WARNING 'Surgery % starting without required consents!', NEW.id;
            INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
                notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
                message, priority, created_at
            ) VALUES (
                'missing_consent', NEW.id, 'clinical.surgeries',
                'WARNING: Surgery starting without signed consents!',
                'urgent', NOW()
            );
        END IF;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_consent_check
BEFORE UPDATE ON clinical.surgeries
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.check_consent_before_surgery();
```

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- OPERATIONS: COMPLAINT & FEEDBACK
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.complaint_categories (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- staff/treatment/billing/cleanliness/food/wait_time/pharmacy/lab
    sla_hours   INTEGER DEFAULT 24,
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.complaints (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    complaint_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Complainant
    patient_id          UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    complainant_name    VARCHAR(200),
    complainant_phone   VARCHAR(20),
    complainant_email   VARCHAR(150),
    complainant_type    VARCHAR(30),    -- patient/attendant/visitor/staff/anonymous
    
    -- Complaint details
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES operations.complaint_categories(id),
    channel             VARCHAR(30),    -- walk_in/phone/online/app/email
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    subject             VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description         TEXT,
    incident_date       DATE,
    department_id       UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id),
    against_staff_id    UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    
    -- Processing
    assigned_to         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    assigned_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    investigation_notes TEXT,
    root_cause          TEXT,
    corrective_action   TEXT,
    
    -- Resolution
    resolved_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    resolved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolution_notes    TEXT,
    sla_breach          BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Feedback
    complainant_satisfied BOOLEAN,
    reopen_count        INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'open',
                        -- open/acknowledged/investigating/resolved/closed/escalated
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- SLA breach check trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.check_complaint_sla()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_sla_hours INTEGER;
BEGIN
    SELECT sla_hours INTO v_sla_hours
    FROM operations.complaint_categories
    WHERE id = NEW.category_id;

    IF NEW.status NOT IN ('resolved', 'closed') THEN
        IF NOW() > (NEW.created_at + (v_sla_hours || ' hours')::INTERVAL) THEN
            NEW.sla_breach := TRUE;
            INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
                notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
                message, priority, created_at
            ) VALUES (
                'sla_breach', NEW.id, 'operations.complaints',
                'SLA breached for complaint: ' || NEW.complaint_no,
                'high', NOW()
            );
        END IF;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_complaint_sla
BEFORE UPDATE ON operations.complaints
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.check_complaint_sla();

CREATE TABLE operations.complaint_timeline (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    complaint_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.complaints(id),
    action          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    notes           TEXT,
    done_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    done_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- FEEDBACK & PATIENT SATISFACTION
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.feedback_forms (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    form_type       VARCHAR(30),    -- discharge/opd/telemedicine/general
    title           VARCHAR(200),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.feedback_questions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    form_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.feedback_forms(id),
    question_text   VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    question_type   VARCHAR(30),    -- rating/yes_no/text/nps
    category        VARCHAR(50),    -- doctor/nurse/facility/food/cleanliness/billing
    sort_order      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_required     BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.patient_feedback (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    form_id         UUID REFERENCES operations.feedback_forms(id),
    patient_id      UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    channel         VARCHAR(30),    -- kiosk/online/whatsapp/paper/qr
    nps_score       INTEGER,        -- 0-10 Net Promoter Score
    overall_rating  DECIMAL(3,1),   -- 1-5
    submitted_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    is_anonymous    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.feedback_responses (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    feedback_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.patient_feedback(id),
    question_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.feedback_questions(id),
    rating_value    INTEGER,        -- for rating questions (1-5)
    text_value      TEXT,           -- for text questions
    boolean_value   BOOLEAN         -- for yes/no questions
);

-- Patient Satisfaction Score View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_patient_satisfaction AS
SELECT
    pf.branch_id,
    b.name AS branch_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at) AS month,
    pf.form_id,
    COUNT(pf.id) AS total_responses,
    ROUND(AVG(pf.overall_rating)::NUMERIC, 2) AS avg_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(pf.nps_score)::NUMERIC, 1) AS avg_nps,
    -- NPS = % promoters (9-10) - % detractors (0-6)
    ROUND(
        (COUNT(CASE WHEN pf.nps_score >= 9 THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
         NULLIF(COUNT(pf.id), 0) * 100) -
        (COUNT(CASE WHEN pf.nps_score <= 6 THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
         NULLIF(COUNT(pf.id), 0) * 100), 1
    ) AS nps_score,
    -- Category ratings
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'doctor'      THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS doctor_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'nurse'       THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS nurse_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'cleanliness' THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS cleanliness_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'food'        THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS food_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'billing'     THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS billing_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'facility'    THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS facility_rating
FROM operations.patient_feedback pf
JOIN platform.branches b ON pf.branch_id = b.id
LEFT JOIN operations.feedback_responses fr ON fr.feedback_id = pf.id
LEFT JOIN operations.feedback_questions fq ON fr.question_id = fq.id
GROUP BY pf.branch_id, b.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at), pf.form_id;
```

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: TELEMEDICINE (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.telemedicine_sessions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    doctor_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    appointment_id      UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    
    -- Session
    session_no          VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    platform            VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'jitsi',   -- jitsi/zoom/custom
    room_name           VARCHAR(200),
    meeting_url         TEXT,
    meeting_password    VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Timing
    scheduled_datetime  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    duration_minutes    INTEGER DEFAULT 15,
    actual_start        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    actual_end          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    actual_duration     INTEGER,
    
    -- Patient inputs
    chief_complaint     TEXT,
    self_reported_vitals JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
                        -- {bp, pulse, temp, spo2, weight}
    
    -- Clinical outputs
    prescription_id     UUID REFERENCES pharmacy.prescriptions(id),
    lab_order_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.lab_orders(id),
    follow_up_date      DATE,
    follow_up_notes     TEXT,
    
    -- Technical
    call_quality        VARCHAR(20),    -- good/fair/poor
    technical_issues    TEXT,
    
    -- Recording
    is_recorded         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    recording_url       TEXT,
    recording_consent   BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    recording_expiry    DATE,
    
    -- Billing
    fee_amount          DECIMAL(12,2),
    bill_id             UUID REFERENCES finance.bills(id),
    payment_status      VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    -- Status
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'scheduled',
                        -- scheduled/waiting/in_progress/completed/cancelled/no_show
    
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- DOCTOR APPOINTMENT — COMPLETE MODULE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.appointment_slots (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    doctor_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    slot_date       DATE NOT NULL,
    start_time      TIME NOT NULL,
    end_time        TIME NOT NULL,
    slot_type       VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'opd',  -- opd/telemedicine
    max_patients    INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    booked_count    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'available', -- available/full/blocked/holiday
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(doctor_id, slot_date, start_time, slot_type)
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.appointments (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    doctor_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    slot_id             UUID REFERENCES clinical.appointment_slots(id),
    appointment_no      VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    appointment_type    VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'opd',
    appointment_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    token_no            INTEGER,
    chief_complaint     TEXT,
    visit_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    booked_by           UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    booking_channel     VARCHAR(30), -- walk_in/phone/online/app
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'booked',
                        -- booked/confirmed/arrived/in_consultation/completed/cancelled/no_show
    arrived_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_start  TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_end    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    wait_time_minutes   INTEGER,
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Update slot booked count
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.update_slot_count()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' AND NEW.slot_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE clinical.appointment_slots
        SET booked_count = booked_count + 1,
            status = CASE
                WHEN (booked_count + 1) >= max_patients THEN 'full'
                ELSE status
            END
        WHERE id = NEW.slot_id;
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.status = 'cancelled'
          AND OLD.status != 'cancelled' AND NEW.slot_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE clinical.appointment_slots
        SET booked_count = GREATEST(booked_count - 1, 0),
            status = CASE
                WHEN status = 'full' THEN 'available'
                ELSE status
            END
        WHERE id = NEW.slot_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_slot_count
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON clinical.appointments
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.update_slot_count();
```

-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.visitor_logs (

    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),

CREATE TABLE operations.waste_collection_logs (

    category_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.waste_categories(id),

    collection_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,

    bags_count      INTEGER,
    collected_by    UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    disposed_by     VARCHAR(200),

    vehicle_no      VARCHAR(30),
    manifest_no     VARCHAR(50),
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- OPERATIONS: BLOOD BANK (Extended)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.blood_bank_inventory (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    blood_group     VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,  -- A+/A-/B+/B-/O+/O-/AB+/AB-
    component       VARCHAR(50),           -- whole_blood/packed_rbcs/ffp/platelets/cryo
    bag_no          VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    donor_id        UUID,
    collection_date DATE,
    expiry_date     DATE NOT NULL,
    volume_ml       INTEGER,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'available',
                    -- available/reserved/issued/discarded/expired
    stored_at       VARCHAR(50),

    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.blood_requests (

    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    requested_by    UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    blood_group     VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    component       VARCHAR(50),
    units_required  INTEGER NOT NULL,
    units_issued    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    urgency         VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'routine',  -- routine/urgent/emergency
    clinical_reason TEXT,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.blood_issues (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    request_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.blood_requests(id),
    bag_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.blood_bank_inventory(id),
    issued_to       UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    issued_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    crossmatch_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    crossmatch_result VARCHAR(20),
    transfusion_start TIMESTAMPTZ,
    transfusion_end   TIMESTAMPTZ,
    reaction_noted  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    reaction_type   VARCHAR(200),
    notes           TEXT
);

-- Auto update blood bag status on issue
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.update_blood_bag_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE operations.blood_bank_inventory
    SET status = 'issued'
    WHERE id = NEW.bag_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_blood_issue_status
AFTER INSERT ON operations.blood_issues
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.update_blood_bag_status();
```

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: CHART OF ACCOUNTS (COA)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.account_groups (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    type        VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,   -- asset/liability/equity/income/expense
    parent_id   UUID REFERENCES finance.account_groups(id),
    sort_order  INTEGER DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE TABLE finance.chart_of_accounts (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    account_group_id UUID REFERENCES finance.account_groups(id),
    account_code    VARCHAR(20) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    account_name    VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    account_type    VARCHAR(30),    -- asset/liability/equity/income/expense
    is_bank_account BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_cash_account BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_control_account BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    parent_id       UUID REFERENCES finance.chart_of_accounts(id),
    balance         DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0,    -- running balance
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.journal_entries (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    entry_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    entry_date      DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    entry_type      VARCHAR(30),    -- payment/receipt/journal/contra
    reference_type  VARCHAR(50),    -- bill/payment/payroll/purchase
    reference_id    UUID,
    description     TEXT,
    total_debit     DECIMAL(16,2),
    total_credit    DECIMAL(16,2),
    is_posted       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    posted_by       UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.journal_entry_lines (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    entry_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.journal_entries(id),
    account_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.chart_of_accounts(id),
    description     VARCHAR(300),
    debit_amount    DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0,
    credit_amount   DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0,
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    cost_center_id  UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id)
);

-- Validate journal entry balanced trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.validate_journal_entry()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_total_debit  DECIMAL;
    v_total_credit DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    SELECT
        COALESCE(SUM(debit_amount), 0),
        COALESCE(SUM(credit_amount), 0)
    INTO v_total_debit, v_total_credit
    FROM finance.journal_entry_lines
    WHERE entry_id = NEW.entry_id;

    IF ABS(v_total_debit - v_total_credit) > 0.01 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Journal entry % is not balanced. Debit: %, Credit: %',
            NEW.entry_id, v_total_debit, v_total_credit;
    END IF;

    UPDATE finance.journal_entries
    SET total_debit = v_total_debit, total_credit = v_total_credit
    WHERE id = NEW.entry_id;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_journal_balance
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON finance.journal_entry_lines
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.validate_journal_entry();

-- Update account balance on journal post
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.update_account_balance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.is_posted = TRUE AND OLD.is_posted = FALSE THEN
        UPDATE finance.chart_of_accounts coa
        SET balance = balance + jel.debit_amount - jel.credit_amount
        FROM finance.journal_entry_lines jel
        WHERE jel.entry_id = NEW.id AND jel.account_id = coa.id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_account_balance
AFTER UPDATE ON finance.journal_entries
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.update_account_balance();

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: ASSET MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.asset_categories (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    depreciation_method VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'straight_line',
    useful_life_years INTEGER,
    salvage_pct     DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE finance.fixed_assets (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES finance.asset_categories(id),
    asset_code          VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    asset_name          VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    purchase_date       DATE,
    purchase_price      DECIMAL(14,2),
    salvage_value       DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    useful_life_years   INTEGER,
    depreciation_method VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'straight_line',
    accumulated_depreciation DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    book_value          DECIMAL(14,2),
    location_id         UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    condition           VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'good',
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active',
    disposal_date       DATE,
    disposal_value      DECIMAL(14,2),
    disposal_reason     TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-compute book value
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.compute_asset_book_value()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.book_value := NEW.purchase_price - COALESCE(NEW.accumulated_depreciation, 0);
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_asset_book_value
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON finance.fixed_assets
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.compute_asset_book_value();

CREATE TABLE finance.asset_depreciations (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    asset_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.fixed_assets(id),
    fiscal_year     INTEGER NOT NULL,
    period_month    INTEGER,
    depreciation    DECIMAL(14,2),
    accumulated     DECIMAL(14,2),
    book_value      DECIMAL(14,2),
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(asset_id, fiscal_year, period_month)
);
```
