## 🔵 ADDITIONAL STORED PROCEDURES

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SP 11: SUPPLY CHAIN PROCUREMENT REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_procurement_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    po_no           TEXT,
    po_date         DATE,
    supplier_name   TEXT,
    total_items     BIGINT,
    po_amount       NUMERIC,
    received_value  NUMERIC,
    pending_value   NUMERIC,
    po_status       TEXT,
    grn_count       BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        po.po_no,
        po.po_date,
        s.name                                              AS supplier_name,
        COUNT(poi.id)::BIGINT                              AS total_items,
        ROUND(po.net_amount::NUMERIC, 2)                   AS po_amount,
        ROUND(SUM(poi.received_qty * poi.unit_price)::NUMERIC, 2) AS received_value,
        ROUND(SUM(poi.pending_qty * poi.unit_price)::NUMERIC, 2)  AS pending_value,
        po.status                                           AS po_status,
        COUNT(DISTINCT grn.id)::BIGINT                     AS grn_count
    FROM operations.purchase_orders po
    JOIN finance.suppliers s ON po.supplier_id = s.id
    JOIN operations.purchase_order_items poi ON poi.po_id = po.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.goods_receipt_notes grn ON grn.po_id = po.id
    WHERE po.branch_id IN (
        SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id
    )
    AND po.po_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY po.po_no, po.po_date, s.name, po.net_amount, po.status
    ORDER BY po.po_date DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 12: STOCK CONSUMPTION ANALYSIS
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_stock_consumption_report(
    p_store_id      UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    item_code       TEXT,
    item_name       TEXT,
    category        TEXT,
    opening_qty     NUMERIC,
    received_qty    NUMERIC,
    issued_qty      NUMERIC,
    closing_qty     NUMERIC,
    consumption_value NUMERIC,
    abc_class       TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH consumption AS (
        SELECT
            sl.item_id,
            SUM(CASE WHEN sl.transaction_type = 'grn' THEN sl.in_qty ELSE 0 END) AS received,
            SUM(CASE WHEN sl.transaction_type IN ('issue', 'transfer_out') THEN sl.out_qty ELSE 0 END) AS issued,
            SUM(sl.in_qty * COALESCE(sl.unit_cost, 0) -
                sl.out_qty * COALESCE(sl.unit_cost, 0)) AS value
        FROM operations.stock_ledger sl
        WHERE sl.store_id = p_store_id
          AND sl.transaction_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
        GROUP BY sl.item_id
    ),
    ranked AS (
        SELECT *,
               NTILE(3) OVER (ORDER BY value DESC) AS tile
        FROM consumption
    )
    SELECT
        im.item_code,
        im.item_name,
        ic.name                                             AS category,
        COALESCE(cs.current_qty + r.issued - r.received, 0)::NUMERIC AS opening_qty,
        ROUND(r.received::NUMERIC, 2)                       AS received_qty,
        ROUND(r.issued::NUMERIC, 2)                         AS issued_qty,
        ROUND(cs.current_qty::NUMERIC, 2)                   AS closing_qty,
        ROUND(r.value::NUMERIC, 2)                          AS consumption_value,
        CASE r.tile WHEN 1 THEN 'A' WHEN 2 THEN 'B' ELSE 'C' END AS abc_class
    FROM ranked r
    JOIN operations.item_master im ON r.item_id = im.id
    JOIN operations.item_categories ic ON im.category_id = ic.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.mv_current_stock cs ON cs.item_id = r.item_id AND cs.store_id = p_store_id
    ORDER BY r.value DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 13: HR ATTENDANCE SUMMARY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_attendance_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_month         INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_year          INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_department_id UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    employee_code   TEXT,
    employee_name   TEXT,
    department      TEXT,
    designation     TEXT,
    total_days      INTEGER,
    present_days    BIGINT,
    absent_days     BIGINT,
    late_days       BIGINT,
    leave_days      NUMERIC,
    overtime_hours  NUMERIC,
    attendance_pct  NUMERIC
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_total_days INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Get working days in month
    v_total_days := 26;

    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        e.employee_code,
        e.full_name                                         AS employee_name,
        dep.name                                            AS department,
        des.name                                            AS designation,
        v_total_days                                        AS total_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'present' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS present_days,
        (v_total_days - COUNT(att.id))::BIGINT              AS absent_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'late' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS late_days,
        COALESCE(SUM(la.days), 0)::NUMERIC                  AS leave_days,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(att.overtime_hours), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS overtime_hours,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'present' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            v_total_days * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS attendance_pct
    FROM hr.employees e
    JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
    JOIN hr.designations des ON e.designation_id = des.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.attendance att ON att.employee_id = e.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM att.attendance_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM att.attendance_date) = p_year
    LEFT JOIN hr.leave_applications la ON la.employee_id = e.id
        AND la.status = 'approved'
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM la.from_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM la.from_date) = p_year
    WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id
      AND e.status = 'active'
      AND (p_department_id IS NULL OR e.department_id = p_department_id)
    GROUP BY e.employee_code, e.full_name, dep.name, des.name
    ORDER BY dep.name, e.full_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 14: MAINTENANCE EQUIPMENT STATUS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_equipment_status_report(
    p_branch_id     UUID,
    p_include_stats BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    equipment_name  TEXT,
    category        TEXT,
    serial_no       TEXT,
    location        TEXT,
    status          TEXT,
    last_maintained DATE,
    next_pm_due     DATE,
    calibration_due DATE,
    warranty_expiry DATE,
    amc_expiry      DATE,
    ytd_breakdown   BIGINT,
    ytd_downtime_hrs NUMERIC,
    ytd_maint_cost  NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        er.equipment_name,
        er.category,
        er.serial_no,
        w.name                                              AS location,
        er.status,
        (SELECT MAX(mwo.completed_at::DATE) FROM facility.maintenance_work_orders mwo
         WHERE mwo.equipment_id = er.id AND mwo.status = 'completed') AS last_maintained,
        (SELECT MIN(ms.next_due_date) FROM facility.maintenance_schedules ms
         WHERE ms.equipment_id = er.id AND ms.is_active = TRUE) AS next_pm_due,
        er.next_calibration                                 AS calibration_due,
        er.warranty_expiry,
        er.amc_end_date                                     AS amc_expiry,
        -- YTD stats
        COUNT(CASE WHEN mwo2.wo_type = 'breakdown'
              AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM mwo2.reported_at) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
              THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS ytd_breakdown,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(YEAR FROM mwo2.reported_at) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
              THEN COALESCE(mwo2.downtime_hours, 0) ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS ytd_downtime_hrs,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(YEAR FROM mwo2.reported_at) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
              THEN COALESCE(mwo2.total_cost, 0) ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS ytd_maint_cost
    FROM facility.equipment_registry er
    LEFT JOIN facility.wards w ON er.location_ward_id = w.id
    LEFT JOIN facility.maintenance_work_orders mwo2 ON mwo2.equipment_id = er.id
    WHERE er.branch_id = p_branch_id AND er.status != 'condemned'
    GROUP BY er.equipment_name, er.category, er.serial_no, w.name,
             er.status, er.next_calibration, er.warranty_expiry, er.amc_end_date, er.id
    ORDER BY er.category, er.equipment_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 15: BLOOD BANK INVENTORY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_blood_bank_report(
    p_branch_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    blood_group         TEXT,
    component           TEXT,
    available_units     BIGINT,
    expiring_7_days     BIGINT,
    expiring_30_days    BIGINT,
    expired_units       BIGINT,
    issued_this_month   BIGINT,
    requests_pending    BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        bbi.blood_group,
        bbi.component,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS available_units,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available'
              AND bbi.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 7 THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS expiring_7_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available'
              AND bbi.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 30 THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS expiring_30_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available'
              AND bbi.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS expired_units,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'issued'
              AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM bi.issued_at) = EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NOW()) THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS issued_this_month,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM operations.blood_requests br2
         WHERE br2.branch_id = p_branch_id
           AND br2.blood_group = bbi.blood_group
           AND br2.component = bbi.component
           AND br2.status = 'pending')::BIGINT AS requests_pending
    FROM operations.blood_bank_inventory bbi
    LEFT JOIN operations.blood_issues bi ON bi.bag_id = bbi.id
    WHERE bbi.branch_id = p_branch_id
    GROUP BY bbi.blood_group, bbi.component
    ORDER BY bbi.blood_group, bbi.component;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 16: COMPLAINT ANALYSIS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_complaint_analysis(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    category_name       TEXT,
    total_complaints    BIGINT,
    resolved            BIGINT,
    pending             BIGINT,
    escalated           BIGINT,
    sla_breached        BIGINT,
    avg_resolution_hrs  NUMERIC,
    satisfaction_rate   NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        cc.name                                             AS category_name,
        COUNT(c.id)::BIGINT                                AS total_complaints,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.status IN ('resolved', 'closed') THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS resolved,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.status = 'open' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS pending,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.status = 'escalated' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS escalated,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.sla_breach = TRUE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS sla_breached,
        ROUND(AVG(
            CASE WHEN c.resolved_at IS NOT NULL
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (c.resolved_at - c.created_at)) / 3600
            END
        )::NUMERIC, 1)                                     AS avg_resolution_hrs,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN c.complainant_satisfied = TRUE THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(CASE WHEN c.complainant_satisfied IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                         AS satisfaction_rate
    FROM operations.complaints c
    JOIN operations.complaint_categories cc ON c.category_id = cc.id
    WHERE c.org_id = p_org_id
      AND c.created_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY cc.name
    ORDER BY total_complaints DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 17: TRIAL BALANCE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_trial_balance(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_as_of     DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    account_code    TEXT,
    account_name    TEXT,
    account_type    TEXT,
    debit_balance   NUMERIC,
    credit_balance  NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        coa.account_code,
        coa.account_name,
        coa.account_type,
        CASE WHEN coa.balance >= 0 THEN ROUND(coa.balance::NUMERIC, 2) ELSE 0 END AS debit_balance,
        CASE WHEN coa.balance < 0  THEN ROUND(ABS(coa.balance)::NUMERIC, 2) ELSE 0 END AS credit_balance
    FROM finance.chart_of_accounts coa
    WHERE coa.org_id = p_org_id
      AND coa.is_active = TRUE
      AND coa.balance != 0
    ORDER BY coa.account_code;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 18: MULTI-BRANCH COMPARISON REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_branch_comparison(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_month     INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_year      INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    branch_name         TEXT,
    total_admissions    BIGINT,
    total_discharges    BIGINT,
    avg_bed_occupancy   NUMERIC,
    opd_visits          BIGINT,
    total_revenue       NUMERIC,
    total_collected     NUMERIC,
    pharmacy_revenue    NUMERIC,
    lab_revenue         NUMERIC,
    total_expenses      NUMERIC,
    net_income          NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        br.name                                             AS branch_name,
        -- Clinical
        COUNT(DISTINCT a.id)::BIGINT AS total_admissions,
        COUNT(DISTINCT d.id)::BIGINT AS total_discharges,
        ROUND(AVG(
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM facility.beds b2
             JOIN facility.wards w2 ON b2.ward_id = w2.id
             WHERE w2.branch_id = br.id AND b2.status = 'occupied')::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM facility.beds b3
                    JOIN facility.wards w3 ON b3.ward_id = w3.id
                    WHERE w3.branch_id = br.id AND b3.is_active = TRUE), 0) * 100
        )::NUMERIC, 1) AS avg_bed_occupancy,
        COUNT(DISTINCT pv.id) FILTER (WHERE pv.visit_type = 'opd')::BIGINT AS opd_visits,
        -- Finance
        ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 0) AS total_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount)::NUMERIC, 0) AS total_collected,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'pharmacy' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 0) AS pharmacy_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'lab' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 0) AS lab_revenue,
        0::NUMERIC AS total_expenses,  -- from journal entries
        ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount)::NUMERIC, 0) AS net_income
    FROM platform.branches br
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.branch_id = br.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM a.admission_datetime) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM a.admission_datetime) = p_year
    LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM d.discharge_datetime) = p_month
    LEFT JOIN clinical.patient_visits pv ON pv.branch_id = br.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM pv.visit_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM pv.visit_date) = p_year
    LEFT JOIN finance.bills b ON b.branch_id = br.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM b.bill_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM b.bill_date) = p_year
        AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND br.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY br.id, br.name
    ORDER BY total_revenue DESC NULLS LAST;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## 📊 COMPLETE FINAL SUMMARY

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SYSTEM HEALTH CHECK FUNCTION
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_system_health_check(p_org_id UUID)
RETURNS TABLE (
    check_name      TEXT,
    status          TEXT,
    count_value     BIGINT,
    action_needed   TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY

    -- Expired medicines
    SELECT 'Expired Medicines'::TEXT, '🔴'::TEXT,
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Discard expired stock immediately'::TEXT
    FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
    JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON ms.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE AND ms.quantity > 0

    UNION ALL

    -- Pending critical lab results
    SELECT 'Unnotified Critical Results', '🔴',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Notify doctors immediately'
    FROM clinical.lab_results lr
    JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON lr.order_item_id IN (
        SELECT id FROM clinical.lab_order_items WHERE order_id = lo.id
    )
    JOIN platform.branches br ON lo.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND lr.is_critical = TRUE
      AND lr.critical_notified_at IS NULL
      AND lr.entered_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'

    UNION ALL

    -- Overdue maintenance
    SELECT 'Overdue PM Schedules', '🟡',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Schedule preventive maintenance'
    FROM facility.maintenance_schedules ms
    JOIN facility.equipment_registry er ON ms.equipment_id = er.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON er.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND ms.next_due_date < CURRENT_DATE
      AND ms.is_active = TRUE

    UNION ALL

    -- SLA breached complaints
    SELECT 'SLA Breached Complaints', '🟡',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Escalate unresolved complaints'
    FROM operations.complaints c
    WHERE c.org_id = p_org_id
      AND c.sla_breach = TRUE
      AND c.status NOT IN ('resolved', 'closed')

    UNION ALL

    -- Missing consents for today surgeries
    SELECT 'Surgeries Without Consent', '🔴',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Obtain patient consent before proceeding'
    FROM clinical.surgeries s
    WHERE s.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
      AND s.scheduled_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
      AND s.status = 'scheduled'
      AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM clinical.patient_consents pc
          WHERE pc.surgery_id = s.id AND pc.is_withdrawn = FALSE
      )

    UNION ALL

    -- Beds in cleaning > 2 hours
    SELECT 'Beds Overdue Cleaning', '🟡',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Follow up with housekeeping'
    FROM facility.bed_cleaning_tasks bct
    JOIN facility.beds b ON bct.bed_id = b.id
    JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
    WHERE w.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
      AND bct.status = 'pending'
      AND bct.assigned_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '2 hours'

    ORDER BY 1;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## 🔵 MISSING VIEWS & ADVANCED ANALYTICS

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- ANALYTICS: ADVANCED CLINICAL VIEWS
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Real-Time ICU Dashboard View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_icu_dashboard AS
SELECT
    w.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    w.id                                                AS ward_id,
    w.name                                              AS ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    COUNT(b.id)                                         AS total_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)   AS occupied_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'available' THEN 1 END)  AS available_beds,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
        NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 1
    )                                                   AS occupancy_pct,
    -- Average vitals of current patients
    ROUND(AVG(
        CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN (
            SELECT vs.spo2 FROM clinical.vital_signs vs
            WHERE vs.patient_id = b.current_patient_id
            ORDER BY vs.recorded_at DESC LIMIT 1
        ) END
    )::NUMERIC, 1)                                      AS avg_spo2,
    ROUND(AVG(
        CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN (
            SELECT vs.pulse_rate FROM clinical.vital_signs vs
            WHERE vs.patient_id = b.current_patient_id
            ORDER BY vs.recorded_at DESC LIMIT 1
        ) END
    )::NUMERIC, 0)                                      AS avg_pulse,
    -- Patients on ventilator (from notes)
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' AND
        EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM clinical.nursing_care_plans ncp
            WHERE ncp.patient_id = b.current_patient_id
              AND ncp.problem ILIKE '%ventilator%'
              AND ncp.status = 'active'
        ) THEN 1 END
    )                                                   AS ventilator_patients,
    -- Avg length of ICU stay
    ROUND(AVG(
        CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN
            EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - (
                SELECT a.admission_datetime FROM clinical.admissions a
                WHERE a.id = b.current_admission_id
            ))
        END
    )::NUMERIC, 1)                                      AS avg_icu_stay_days
FROM facility.wards w
JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
JOIN facility.beds b ON b.ward_id = w.id AND b.is_active = TRUE
WHERE w.ward_type IN ('icu', 'nicu', 'ccu', 'picu')
GROUP BY w.branch_id, br.name, w.id, w.name, w.ward_type;

-- ============================================================
-- 2. OPD Queue Real-Time View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_opd_queue AS
SELECT
    a.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    a.doctor_id,
    e.full_name                                         AS doctor_name,
    d.specialization,
    COUNT(a.id)                                         AS total_today,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'booked' THEN 1 END)     AS waiting,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'arrived' THEN 1 END)    AS arrived,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'in_consultation' THEN 1 END) AS in_consultation,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END)  AS completed,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'no_show' THEN 1 END)    AS no_show,
    MIN(a.appointment_datetime)                         AS first_slot,
    MAX(a.appointment_datetime)                         AS last_slot,
    ROUND(AVG(a.wait_time_minutes)::NUMERIC, 0)         AS avg_wait_min,
    -- Next patient
    (SELECT p.full_name FROM clinical.appointments a2
     JOIN clinical.patients p ON a2.patient_id = p.id
     WHERE a2.doctor_id = a.doctor_id
       AND a2.appointment_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
       AND a2.status = 'arrived'
     ORDER BY a2.appointment_datetime LIMIT 1)          AS next_patient
FROM clinical.appointments a
JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
JOIN hr.doctors d ON a.doctor_id = d.id
JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
WHERE a.appointment_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
GROUP BY a.branch_id, br.name, a.doctor_id, e.full_name, d.specialization;

-- ============================================================
-- 3. Emergency Dashboard View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_emergency_dashboard AS
SELECT
    v.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('hour', v.visit_date)                   AS hour_block,
    COUNT(v.id)                                         AS total_visits,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN v.status = 'active' THEN 1 END)     AS currently_active,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN v.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END)  AS discharged,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END)        AS admitted_from_er,
    -- Average time to first doctor seen
    ROUND(AVG(
        EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (cn.note_date - v.visit_date)) / 60
    )::NUMERIC, 0)                                      AS avg_time_to_doctor_min,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN vs.spo2 < 90 THEN 1 END)           AS critical_spo2_count,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN vs.bp_systolic > 180 THEN 1 END)   AS hypertensive_crisis_count
FROM clinical.patient_visits v
JOIN platform.branches br ON v.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.visit_id = v.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.clinical_notes cn ON cn.visit_id = v.id
    AND cn.note_type = 'progress'
LEFT JOIN clinical.vital_signs vs ON vs.visit_id = v.id
WHERE v.visit_type = 'emergency'
  AND v.visit_date >= CURRENT_DATE
GROUP BY v.branch_id, br.name, DATE_TRUNC('hour', v.visit_date)
ORDER BY hour_block DESC;

-- ============================================================
-- 4. Medication Safety View (Drug Interactions Alert)
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_active_medication_orders AS
SELECT
    mo.patient_id,
    p.full_name                                         AS patient_name,
    p.mrn,
    a.admission_no,
    b.bed_no,
    w.name                                              AS ward_name,
    mo.id                                               AS order_id,
    mo.drug_name,
    mo.generic_name,
    mo.dose,
    mo.route,
    mo.frequency,
    mo.start_datetime,
    mo.end_datetime,
    mo.is_stat,
    mo.is_prn,
    mo.is_narcotic,
    mo.is_high_alert      -- from extended medicine info
        -- join pharmacy.medicines where needed
    u.full_name                                         AS ordered_by,
    mo.ordered_at,
    -- Last administration
    (SELECT ma.administered_at FROM clinical.medication_administrations ma
     WHERE ma.order_id = mo.id ORDER BY ma.administered_at DESC LIMIT 1) AS last_given,
    -- Next due
    mo.status
FROM clinical.medication_orders mo
JOIN clinical.patients p ON mo.patient_id = p.id
JOIN clinical.admissions a ON mo.admission_id = a.id
JOIN facility.beds b ON a.bed_id = b.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
JOIN platform.users u ON mo.ordered_by = u.id
WHERE mo.status = 'active';

-- ============================================================
-- 5. Pending Doctor Orders View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_pending_orders AS
WITH pending_labs AS (
    SELECT
        lo.patient_id,
        lo.branch_id,
        lo.admission_id,
        'lab_order'::TEXT AS order_type,
        tm.test_name AS item_name,
        lo.priority,
        lo.ordered_at,
        lo.status
    FROM clinical.lab_orders lo
    JOIN clinical.lab_order_items loi ON loi.order_id = lo.id
    JOIN clinical.test_master tm ON loi.test_id = tm.id
    WHERE lo.status IN ('pending', 'collected', 'processing')
),
pending_meds AS (
    SELECT
        mo.patient_id,
        a.branch_id,
        mo.admission_id,
        'medication'::TEXT AS order_type,
        mo.drug_name AS item_name,
        CASE WHEN mo.is_stat THEN 'stat' ELSE 'routine' END AS priority,
        mo.ordered_at,
        mo.status
    FROM clinical.medication_orders mo
    JOIN clinical.admissions a ON mo.admission_id = a.id
    WHERE mo.status = 'active'
      AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM clinical.medication_administrations ma
          WHERE ma.order_id = mo.id
            AND ma.administered_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '8 hours'
      )
)
SELECT
    po.*,
    p.full_name AS patient_name,
    p.mrn,
    w.name AS ward_name,
    b.bed_no
FROM (
    SELECT * FROM pending_labs
    UNION ALL
    SELECT * FROM pending_meds
) po
JOIN clinical.patients p ON po.patient_id = p.id
JOIN clinical.admissions adm ON po.admission_id = adm.id
JOIN facility.beds b ON adm.bed_id = b.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
ORDER BY po.priority DESC, po.ordered_at ASC;

-- ============================================================
-- 6. Revenue Breakdown by Service View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_revenue_by_service AS
SELECT
    bi.bill_id,
    b.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    b.bill_date::DATE                                   AS bill_date,
    b.patient_id,
    b.admission_id,
    b.bill_type,
    bi.item_type,
    bi.item_name,
    bi.quantity,
    bi.unit_price,
    bi.discount_amount,
    bi.net_amount,
    b.status                                            AS bill_status,
    -- Doctor/department attribution
    CASE bi.item_type
        WHEN 'bed'       THEN 'Ward Revenue'
        WHEN 'lab'       THEN 'Lab Revenue'
        WHEN 'radiology' THEN 'Radiology Revenue'
        WHEN 'medicine'  THEN 'Pharmacy Revenue'
        WHEN 'ot'        THEN 'OT Revenue'
        WHEN 'doctor_fee' THEN 'Doctor Fee Revenue'
        WHEN 'procedure' THEN 'Procedure Revenue'
        ELSE                  'Other Revenue'
    END                                                 AS revenue_category,
    bi.service_date
FROM finance.bill_items bi
JOIN finance.bills b ON bi.bill_id = b.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
WHERE b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft');

-- ============================================================
-- 7. Nurse Workload View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_nurse_workload AS
SELECT
    ns.nurse_id,
    e.full_name                                         AS nurse_name,
    ns.ward_id,
    w.name                                              AS ward_name,
    ns.shift_type,
    ns.shift_date,
    -- Patient count in ward during shift
    COUNT(DISTINCT a.id)                                AS assigned_patients,
    -- Medication administrations done
    COUNT(DISTINCT ma.id)                               AS meds_administered,
    -- Vitals recorded
    COUNT(DISTINCT vs.id)                               AS vitals_recorded,
    -- Nursing notes written
    COUNT(DISTINCT cn.id)                               AS notes_written,
    -- Overdue medications
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN mo.is_stat = TRUE
        AND NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM clinical.medication_administrations ma2
            WHERE ma2.order_id = mo.id
              AND ma2.administered_at > ns.shift_date::TIMESTAMPTZ
        ) THEN mo.id END
    )                                                   AS overdue_stat_meds
FROM clinical.nurse_shifts ns
JOIN hr.employees e ON ns.nurse_id = e.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON ns.ward_id = w.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.ward_id = ns.ward_id
    AND a.status = 'admitted'
    AND a.admission_datetime::DATE <= ns.shift_date
LEFT JOIN clinical.medication_administrations ma ON ma.administered_by = ns.nurse_id
    AND ma.administered_at::DATE = ns.shift_date
LEFT JOIN clinical.vital_signs vs ON vs.recorded_by = ns.nurse_id
    AND vs.recorded_at::DATE = ns.shift_date
LEFT JOIN clinical.clinical_notes cn ON cn.authored_by = ns.nurse_id
    AND cn.note_date::DATE = ns.shift_date
LEFT JOIN clinical.medication_orders mo ON mo.admission_id = a.id
    AND mo.status = 'active' AND mo.is_stat = TRUE
GROUP BY ns.nurse_id, e.full_name, ns.ward_id, w.name, ns.shift_type, ns.shift_date;

-- ============================================================
-- 8. Financial Aging Report View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_receivables_aging AS
SELECT
    b.org_id,
    b.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    p.id                                                AS patient_id,
    p.full_name                                         AS patient_name,
    p.mrn,
    b.bill_no,
    b.bill_date,
    b.net_amount,
    b.paid_amount,
    b.insurance_amount,
    b.balance_amount,
    CURRENT_DATE - b.bill_date::DATE                   AS days_outstanding,
    CASE
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - b.bill_date::DATE <= 30  THEN '0-30 Days'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - b.bill_date::DATE <= 60  THEN '31-60 Days'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - b.bill_date::DATE <= 90  THEN '61-90 Days'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - b.bill_date::DATE <= 180 THEN '91-180 Days'
        ELSE                                               '180+ Days'
    END                                                 AS aging_bucket,
    b.bill_type,
    ic.name                                             AS insurance_company,
    b.status
FROM finance.bills b
JOIN clinical.patients p ON b.patient_id = p.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN finance.patient_insurance_policies pip ON b.insurance_id = pip.id
LEFT JOIN finance.insurance_companies ic ON pip.company_id = ic.id
WHERE b.balance_amount > 0
  AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft');

-- ============================================================
-- 9. Staff Performance Scorecard View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_staff_scorecard AS
SELECT
    e.id                                                AS employee_id,
    e.full_name,
    e.employee_code,
    dep.name                                            AS department,
    des.name                                            AS designation,
    -- Attendance score
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'present' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
        NULLIF(COUNT(att.id), 0) * 100, 1
    )                                                   AS attendance_pct,
    -- Leave utilization
    COALESCE((
        SELECT SUM(la.days) FROM hr.leave_applications la
        WHERE la.employee_id = e.id
          AND la.status = 'approved'
          AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM la.from_date) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
    ), 0)                                               AS leaves_taken_ytd,
    -- Overtime
    ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(att.overtime_hours), 0)::NUMERIC, 1) AS overtime_hrs,
    -- Complaints against
    COALESCE((
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM operations.complaints c
        WHERE c.against_staff_id = e.id
          AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM c.created_at) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
    ), 0)                                               AS complaints_against,
    -- Training completed
    0                                                   AS trainings_completed  -- placeholder
FROM hr.employees e
JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
JOIN hr.designations des ON e.designation_id = des.id
LEFT JOIN hr.attendance att ON att.employee_id = e.id
    AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM att.attendance_date) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
WHERE e.status = 'active'
GROUP BY e.id, e.full_name, e.employee_code, dep.name, des.name;

-- ============================================================
-- 10. Discharge Readiness View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_discharge_readiness AS
SELECT
    a.id                                                AS admission_id,
    a.branch_id,
    p.full_name                                         AS patient_name,
    p.mrn,
    b.bed_no,
    w.name                                              AS ward_name,
    a.admission_datetime,
    EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - a.admission_datetime)     AS current_los,
    a.admitting_doctor_id,
    e.full_name                                         AS doctor_name,
    -- Checklist items
    CASE WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM clinical.lab_orders lo
        WHERE lo.admission_id = a.id AND lo.status != 'resulted'
    ) THEN FALSE ELSE TRUE END                          AS labs_complete,
    CASE WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM finance.bills bi
        WHERE bi.admission_id = a.id AND bi.status = 'final' AND bi.balance_amount > 0
    ) THEN FALSE ELSE TRUE END                          AS bill_cleared,
    CASE WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pharmacy.prescriptions rx
        WHERE rx.admission_id = a.id AND rx.prescription_type = 'discharge'
    ) THEN TRUE ELSE FALSE END                          AS discharge_rx_done,
    CASE WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM clinical.clinical_notes cn
        WHERE cn.admission_id = a.id AND cn.note_type = 'discharge_summary'
          AND cn.is_final = TRUE
    ) THEN TRUE ELSE FALSE END                          AS discharge_summary_done,
    -- Overall readiness
    CASE WHEN
        (NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clinical.lab_orders lo WHERE lo.admission_id = a.id AND lo.status != 'resulted'))
        AND
        (NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM finance.bills bi WHERE bi.admission_id = a.id AND bi.status = 'final' AND bi.balance_amount > 0))
    THEN TRUE ELSE FALSE END                            AS ready_for_discharge,
    a.status
FROM clinical.admissions a
JOIN clinical.patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id
JOIN facility.beds b ON a.bed_id = b.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
JOIN hr.doctors d ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
WHERE a.status = 'admitted'
ORDER BY a.admission_datetime ASC;

-- ============================================================
-- 11. Reagent & Lab QC View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_lab_qc_status AS
SELECT
    b.id                                                AS branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    tm.test_name,
    tm.test_code,
    tm.category_id,
    -- QC last done (from maintenance log concept)
    -- Using a placeholder - needs reagent_management table
    NOW()::DATE                                         AS qc_last_done,
    -- TAT compliance
    COUNT(lr.id)                                        AS total_results_today,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.is_critical THEN 1 END)          AS critical_results,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.abnormal_flag IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) AS abnormal_results,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.status = 'final' THEN 1 END)     AS verified_results,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.status = 'draft' THEN 1 END)     AS pending_verification,
    -- Average TAT
    ROUND(AVG(
        EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.verified_at - lr.entered_at)) / 60
    )::NUMERIC, 0)                                      AS avg_verification_min
FROM platform.branches br
JOIN platform.organizations o ON br.org_id = o.id
JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON lo.branch_id = br.id
    AND lo.ordered_at::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
JOIN clinical.lab_order_items loi ON loi.order_id = lo.id
JOIN clinical.test_master tm ON loi.test_id = tm.id
JOIN clinical.lab_results lr ON lr.order_item_id = loi.id
JOIN platform.branches b ON lo.branch_id = b.id
GROUP BY b.id, br.name, tm.test_name, tm.test_code, tm.category_id;
```

## 🔵 REAGENT MANAGEMENT MODULE

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: REAGENT MANAGEMENT (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.reagent_categories (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.reagents (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES clinical.reagent_categories(id),
    reagent_name        VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    reagent_code        VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE,
    manufacturer        VARCHAR(200),
    catalog_no          VARCHAR(100),
    storage_condition   VARCHAR(100),   -- 2-8C/room_temp/-20C
    unit_of_measure     VARCHAR(30),
    tests_per_kit       INTEGER,        -- how many tests per kit/reagent pack
    instrument_model    VARCHAR(200),   -- compatible analyzer
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.reagent_stock (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    reagent_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.reagents(id),
    lot_no          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    expiry_date     DATE NOT NULL,
    quantity        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,  -- kits/packs
    tests_remaining INTEGER,
    received_date   DATE,
    open_date       DATE,
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    supplier_id     UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    purchase_price  DECIMAL(12,2),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'sealed',  -- sealed/open/empty/expired
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.reagent_usage_log (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    stock_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.reagent_stock(id),
    reagent_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.reagents(id),
    usage_date      DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    tests_run       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    order_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.lab_orders(id),
    used_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    qc_run          BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Reduce reagent on usage
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.reduce_reagent_on_usage()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE clinical.reagent_stock
    SET tests_remaining = GREATEST(COALESCE(tests_remaining, 0) - NEW.tests_run, 0),
        status = CASE
            WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(tests_remaining, 0) - NEW.tests_run, 0) = 0 THEN 'empty'
            ELSE status
        END
    WHERE id = NEW.stock_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_usage
AFTER INSERT ON clinical.reagent_usage_log
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.reduce_reagent_on_usage();

CREATE TABLE clinical.reagent_qc_logs (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    reagent_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.reagents(id),
    stock_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.reagent_stock(id),
    qc_date         DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    qc_level        VARCHAR(20),    -- level1/level2/level3
    expected_value  DECIMAL(15,4),
    obtained_value  DECIMAL(15,4),
    sd_value        DECIMAL(10,4),
    cv_pct          DECIMAL(8,4),
    result          VARCHAR(20),    -- pass/fail/warning
    action_taken    TEXT,
    performed_by    UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Reagent expiry alert trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.alert_reagent_expiry()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 30
       AND OLD.status != 'expired' AND NEW.status != 'expired' THEN
        INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
            notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
            message, priority, created_at
        ) VALUES (
            'reagent_expiry', NEW.id, 'clinical.reagent_stock',
            'Reagent lot ' || NEW.lot_no || ' expires on ' || NEW.expiry_date,
            'high', NOW()
        );
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_expiry
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON clinical.reagent_stock
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.alert_reagent_expiry();
```

## 🔵 AMBULANCE MANAGEMENT MODULE

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- OPERATIONS: AMBULANCE MANAGEMENT (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.ambulances (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    vehicle_no      VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    vehicle_type    VARCHAR(50),    -- basic/advanced/neonatal/cardiac/air
    model           VARCHAR(100),
    capacity        INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    equipment_list  JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    gps_device_id   VARCHAR(100),
    insurance_expiry DATE,
    fitness_expiry  DATE,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'available', -- available/dispatched/maintenance/offline
    current_lat     DECIMAL(10,8),
    current_lng     DECIMAL(11,8),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.ambulance_drivers (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    employee_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    license_no      VARCHAR(50),
    license_expiry  DATE,
    license_type    VARCHAR(30),
    is_available    BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    current_ambulance_id UUID REFERENCES operations.ambulances(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.ambulance_calls (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    call_no             VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    call_datetime       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    call_type           VARCHAR(30),    -- emergency/scheduled/transfer/discharge
    
    -- Caller info
    caller_name         VARCHAR(200),
    caller_phone        VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Patient info
    patient_id          UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    patient_name        VARCHAR(200),   -- if not registered
    patient_condition   TEXT,
    
    -- Location
    pickup_address      TEXT NOT NULL,
    pickup_lat          DECIMAL(10,8),
    pickup_lng          DECIMAL(11,8),
    destination_address TEXT,
    destination_lat     DECIMAL(10,8),
    destination_lng     DECIMAL(11,8),
    
    -- Assignment
    ambulance_id        UUID REFERENCES operations.ambulances(id),
    driver_id           UUID REFERENCES operations.ambulance_drivers(id),
    paramedic_id        UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    
    -- Timings
    dispatched_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    arrived_at_scene    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    departed_scene      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    arrived_hospital    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Distances
    distance_km         DECIMAL(8,2),
    
    -- Clinical
    chief_complaint     TEXT,
    vitals_at_scene     JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    interventions       JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    
    -- Billing
    base_charge         DECIMAL(12,2),
    per_km_charge       DECIMAL(10,2),
    total_charge        DECIMAL(12,2),
    bill_id             UUID REFERENCES finance.bills(id),
    
    -- Status
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'pending',
                        -- pending/dispatched/en_route/at_scene/transporting/completed/cancelled
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-compute ambulance charges
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.compute_ambulance_charge()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_base    DECIMAL := 500.00;    -- configurable
    v_per_km  DECIMAL := 15.00;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.distance_km IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.base_charge := v_base;
        NEW.per_km_charge := v_per_km;
        NEW.total_charge := v_base + (NEW.distance_km * v_per_km);
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_ambulance_charge
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON operations.ambulance_calls
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.compute_ambulance_charge();

-- Update ambulance status on dispatch
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.update_ambulance_on_dispatch()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'dispatched' AND OLD.status = 'pending' THEN
        UPDATE operations.ambulances
        SET status = 'dispatched'
        WHERE id = NEW.ambulance_id;
        
        UPDATE operations.ambulance_drivers
        SET is_available = FALSE
        WHERE id = NEW.driver_id;
    END IF;
    
    IF NEW.status IN ('completed', 'cancelled') THEN
        UPDATE operations.ambulances
        SET status = 'available'
        WHERE id = NEW.ambulance_id;
        
        UPDATE operations.ambulance_drivers
        SET is_available = TRUE
        WHERE id = NEW.driver_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_ambulance_dispatch
AFTER UPDATE ON operations.ambulance_calls
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.update_ambulance_on_dispatch();

CREATE TABLE operations.ambulance_maintenance_log (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ambulance_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.ambulances(id),
    maintenance_type VARCHAR(50),   -- service/repair/inspection
    description     TEXT,
    done_date       DATE NOT NULL,
    next_due_date   DATE,
    odometer_km     INTEGER,
    cost            DECIMAL(12,2),
    done_by         VARCHAR(200),
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- AUDIT: NOTIFICATION ENGINE (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE audit.notification_templates (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    template_code   VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    template_name   VARCHAR(200),
    channel         VARCHAR(30),    -- sms/email/whatsapp/push/system
    subject         VARCHAR(300),   -- for email
    body_en         TEXT NOT NULL,
    body_bn         TEXT,
    -- Variables like {{patient_name}}, {{appointment_date}}
    variables       JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Seed notification templates
INSERT INTO audit.notification_templates (template_code, template_name, channel, subject, body_en) VALUES
('APPT_CONFIRM',    'Appointment Confirmation',  'sms', NULL,
 'Dear {{patient_name}}, your appointment with Dr. {{doctor_name}} is confirmed for {{date}} at {{time}}. Token: {{token}}. Hospital: {{hospital_name}}'),
('APPT_REMINDER',   'Appointment Reminder',      'sms', NULL,
 'Reminder: Your appointment with Dr. {{doctor_name}} is tomorrow at {{time}}. Please arrive 15 mins early.'),
('APPT_CANCELLED',  'Appointment Cancelled',     'sms', NULL,
 'Your appointment with Dr. {{doctor_name}} on {{date}} has been cancelled. Please call us to reschedule.'),
('LAB_RESULT_READY','Lab Result Ready',          'sms', NULL,
 'Dear {{patient_name}}, your lab results are ready. Please collect from reception or view online at {{portal_url}}'),
('CRITICAL_RESULT',  'Critical Lab Result',       'system', NULL,
 'CRITICAL RESULT: Patient {{patient_name}} ({{mrn}}) - {{test_name}}: {{result_value}} {{unit}}'),
('DISCHARGE_SUMMARY','Discharge Summary',         'email', 'Your Discharge Summary - {{hospital_name}}',
 'Dear {{patient_name}}, please find your discharge summary attached. Follow-up date: {{follow_up_date}}'),
('BILL_GENERATED',  'Bill Generated',            'sms', NULL,
 'Your bill of BDT {{amount}} has been generated. Bill No: {{bill_no}}. Please pay at the billing counter.'),
('PAYMENT_RECEIPT', 'Payment Receipt',           'sms', NULL,
 'Payment of BDT {{amount}} received against Bill {{bill_no}}. Balance: BDT {{balance}}. Thank you!'),
('LOW_STOCK_ALERT', 'Low Stock Alert',           'system', NULL,
 'Low stock alert: {{item_name}} in {{store_name}}. Current qty: {{quantity}}. Reorder level: {{reorder_level}}'),
('MAINTENANCE_DUE', 'Maintenance Due',           'system', NULL,
 'Preventive maintenance due for {{equipment_name}} (S/N: {{serial_no}}) on {{due_date}}'),
('LEAVE_APPROVED',  'Leave Approved',            'sms', NULL,
 'Dear {{employee_name}}, your leave from {{from_date}} to {{to_date}} has been approved.'),
('BLOOD_EXPIRY',    'Blood Expiry Alert',        'system', NULL,
 'Blood bag {{bag_no}} ({{blood_group}}) expires on {{expiry_date}}. Take immediate action.');

CREATE TABLE audit.notification_logs (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    org_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    template_id     UUID REFERENCES audit.notification_templates(id),
    queue_id        BIGINT REFERENCES audit.notifications_queue(id),
    recipient_user_id UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    recipient_name  VARCHAR(200),
    recipient_phone VARCHAR(20),
    recipient_email VARCHAR(150),
    channel         VARCHAR(30),
    subject         VARCHAR(300),
    body            TEXT,
    variables_used  JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',  -- pending/sent/failed/delivered
    sent_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    delivered_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message   TEXT,
    retry_count     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Process notification queue function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.process_notification(
    p_queue_id BIGINT
)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
    v_notif     RECORD;
    v_template  RECORD;
    v_body      TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Get queued notification
    SELECT * INTO v_notif
    FROM audit.notifications_queue
    WHERE id = p_queue_id AND is_sent = FALSE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN FALSE;
    END IF;

    -- Get matching template
    SELECT * INTO v_template
    FROM audit.notification_templates
    WHERE template_code = v_notif.notification_type AND is_active = TRUE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        -- No template, mark as processed
        UPDATE audit.notifications_queue SET is_sent = TRUE, sent_at = NOW()
        WHERE id = p_queue_id;
        RETURN FALSE;
    END IF;

    -- Log notification (actual sending via external service/worker)
    INSERT INTO audit.notification_logs(
        template_id, queue_id, channel,
        subject, body, status, created_at
    ) VALUES (
        v_template.id, p_queue_id, v_template.channel,
        v_template.subject, v_template.body_en, 'pending', NOW()
    );

    -- Mark queue as sent
    UPDATE audit.notifications_queue
    SET is_sent = TRUE, sent_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = p_queue_id;

    RETURN TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## 🔵 ADDITIONAL STORED PROCEDURES (Continued)

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SP 19: PATIENT HISTORY FULL REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_patient_history_report(
    p_patient_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    section         TEXT,
    event_date      TEXT,
    description     TEXT,
    details         TEXT,
    recorded_by     TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY

    -- Admissions
    SELECT
        'Admission'::TEXT,
        a.admission_datetime::DATE::TEXT,
        'IPD Admission - ' || a.admission_no,
        'Ward: ' || COALESCE(w.name, 'N/A') ||
        ', Doctor: ' || COALESCE(e.full_name, 'N/A') ||
        ', Diagnosis: ' || COALESCE(a.primary_diagnosis, 'N/A'),
        u.full_name
    FROM clinical.admissions a
    LEFT JOIN facility.wards w ON a.ward_id = w.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.doctors d ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    LEFT JOIN platform.users u ON a.created_by = u.id
    WHERE a.patient_id = p_patient_id

    UNION ALL

    -- Discharges
    SELECT
        'Discharge'::TEXT,
        d.discharge_datetime::DATE::TEXT,
        'Discharged - ' || COALESCE(d.discharge_type, 'Regular'),
        'Condition: ' || COALESCE(d.discharge_condition, 'N/A') ||
        ', Diagnosis: ' || COALESCE(d.final_diagnosis, 'N/A') ||
        ', LOS: ' || COALESCE(
            (SELECT a2.length_of_stay::TEXT FROM clinical.admissions a2
             WHERE a2.id = d.admission_id), 'N/A'
        ) || ' days',
        u.full_name
    FROM clinical.discharges d
    LEFT JOIN platform.users u ON d.discharge_by = u.id
    WHERE d.patient_id = p_patient_id

    UNION ALL

    -- Surgeries
    SELECT
        'Surgery'::TEXT,
        s.scheduled_datetime::DATE::TEXT,
        'Surgery: ' || COALESCE(s.surgery_name, 'N/A'),
        'OT: ' || COALESCE(ot.name, 'N/A') ||
        ', Type: ' || COALESCE(s.anesthesia_type, 'N/A') ||
        ', Duration: ' || COALESCE(s.duration_minutes::TEXT, 'N/A') || ' min' ||
        ', Status: ' || s.status,
        e.full_name
    FROM clinical.surgeries s
    LEFT JOIN clinical.ot_rooms ot ON s.ot_room_id = ot.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.doctors d ON s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    WHERE s.patient_id = p_patient_id

    UNION ALL

    -- Lab Orders
    SELECT
        'Lab Test'::TEXT,
        lo.ordered_at::DATE::TEXT,
        'Lab Order: ' || lo.order_no,
        'Tests: ' || (
            SELECT STRING_AGG(tm.test_name, ', ')
            FROM clinical.lab_order_items loi
            JOIN clinical.test_master tm ON loi.test_id = tm.id
            WHERE loi.order_id = lo.id
        ) || ', Priority: ' || lo.priority,
        u.full_name
    FROM clinical.lab_orders lo
    LEFT JOIN platform.users u ON lo.ordered_by = u.id
    WHERE lo.patient_id = p_patient_id

    UNION ALL

    -- Vital Signs (summarized)
    SELECT
        'Vital Signs'::TEXT,
        vs.recorded_at::DATE::TEXT,
        'Vitals Recorded',
        'BP: ' || COALESCE(vs.bp_systolic::TEXT, '-') || '/' ||
                   COALESCE(vs.bp_diastolic::TEXT, '-') ||
        ', Pulse: ' || COALESCE(vs.pulse_rate::TEXT, '-') ||
        ', Temp: ' || COALESCE(vs.temperature::TEXT, '-') ||
        ', SpO2: ' || COALESCE(vs.spo2::TEXT, '-') || '%',
        u.full_name
    FROM clinical.vital_signs vs
    LEFT JOIN platform.users u ON vs.recorded_by = u.id
    WHERE vs.patient_id = p_patient_id

    UNION ALL

    -- Bills
    SELECT
        'Bill'::TEXT,
        b.bill_date::DATE::TEXT,
        'Bill: ' || b.bill_no,
        'Type: ' || b.bill_type ||
        ', Amount: BDT ' || b.net_amount::TEXT ||
        ', Paid: BDT ' || b.paid_amount::TEXT ||
        ', Status: ' || b.status,
        u.full_name
    FROM finance.bills b
    LEFT JOIN platform.users u ON b.created_by = u.id
    WHERE b.patient_id = p_patient_id

    ORDER BY event_date DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 20: INFECTION CONTROL REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_infection_control_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    metric          TEXT,
    value           NUMERIC,
    unit            TEXT,
    benchmark       NUMERIC,
    status          TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_total_admissions  INTEGER;
    v_total_surgeries   INTEGER;
BEGIN
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_total_admissions
    FROM clinical.admissions a
    WHERE a.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND a.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date;

    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_total_surgeries
    FROM clinical.surgeries s
    WHERE s.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND s.status = 'completed';

    RETURN QUERY

    -- WHO surgical safety checklist compliance
    SELECT
        'WHO Checklist Compliance'::TEXT,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN s.who_sign_in_done AND s.who_time_out_done AND s.who_sign_out_done THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(v_total_surgeries, 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC,
        '%'::TEXT,
        100.0::NUMERIC,
        CASE WHEN ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN s.who_sign_in_done AND s.who_time_out_done AND s.who_sign_out_done THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(v_total_surgeries, 0) * 100, 1
        ) >= 95 THEN '🟢 Good' ELSE '🔴 Needs Attention' END
    FROM clinical.surgeries s
    WHERE s.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND s.status = 'completed'

    UNION ALL

    -- Medication error rate
    SELECT
        'Medication Error Rate'::TEXT,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN ma.status = 'missed' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(ma.id), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC,
        '%'::TEXT,
        1.0::NUMERIC,  -- benchmark: < 1%
        CASE WHEN ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN ma.status = 'missed' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(ma.id), 0) * 100, 2
        ) <= 1 THEN '🟢 Good' ELSE '🔴 Needs Attention' END
    FROM clinical.medication_administrations ma
    JOIN clinical.medication_orders mo ON ma.order_id = mo.id
    JOIN clinical.admissions a ON mo.admission_id = a.id
    WHERE a.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND ma.administered_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- Critical result notification rate
    SELECT
        'Critical Result Notification Rate'::TEXT,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.critical_notified_at IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.is_critical THEN 1 END), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC,
        '%'::TEXT,
        100.0::NUMERIC,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.is_critical AND lr.critical_notified_at IS NULL THEN 1 END) = 0
             THEN '🟢 Good' ELSE '🔴 Needs Attention' END
    FROM clinical.lab_results lr
    JOIN clinical.lab_order_items loi ON lr.order_item_id = loi.id
    JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON loi.order_id = lo.id
    WHERE lo.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND lr.entered_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 21: REAGENT CONSUMPTION REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_reagent_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    reagent_name        TEXT,
    lot_no              TEXT,
    expiry_date         DATE,
    opening_tests       INTEGER,
    tests_consumed      BIGINT,
    qc_tests            BIGINT,
    patient_tests       BIGINT,
    remaining_tests     INTEGER,
    cost_per_test       NUMERIC,
    total_cost          NUMERIC,
    qc_pass_rate        NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        r.reagent_name,
        rs.lot_no,
        rs.expiry_date,
        rs.tests_remaining + COALESCE(SUM(rul.tests_run)::INTEGER, 0) AS opening_tests,
        COALESCE(SUM(rul.tests_run), 0)::BIGINT                       AS tests_consumed,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN rul.qc_run THEN rul.tests_run ELSE 0 END), 0)::BIGINT AS qc_tests,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN NOT rul.qc_run THEN rul.tests_run ELSE 0 END), 0)::BIGINT AS patient_tests,
        rs.tests_remaining,
        ROUND(
            COALESCE(rs.purchase_price, 0) /
            NULLIF(rs.tests_remaining + COALESCE(SUM(rul.tests_run)::INTEGER, 0), 0), 2
        )::NUMERIC                                                     AS cost_per_test,
        ROUND(
            COALESCE(rs.purchase_price, 0) *
            COALESCE(SUM(rul.tests_run), 0) /
            NULLIF(rs.tests_remaining + COALESCE(SUM(rul.tests_run)::INTEGER, 0), 0), 2
        )::NUMERIC                                                     AS total_cost,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN rqc.result = 'pass' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(rqc.id), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                                     AS qc_pass_rate
    FROM clinical.reagent_stock rs
    JOIN clinical.reagents r ON rs.reagent_id = r.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.reagent_usage_log rul ON rul.stock_id = rs.id
        AND rul.usage_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN clinical.reagent_qc_logs rqc ON rqc.stock_id = rs.id
        AND rqc.qc_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    WHERE rs.branch_id = p_branch_id
    GROUP BY r.reagent_name, rs.lot_no, rs.expiry_date,
             rs.tests_remaining, rs.purchase_price
    ORDER BY tests_consumed DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 22: AMBULANCE PERFORMANCE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_ambulance_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    vehicle_no          TEXT,
    vehicle_type        TEXT,
    total_calls         BIGINT,
    completed_calls     BIGINT,
    cancelled_calls     BIGINT,
    avg_response_min    NUMERIC,
    avg_trip_duration   NUMERIC,
    total_distance_km   NUMERIC,
    total_revenue       NUMERIC,
    utilization_pct     NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        am.vehicle_no,
        am.vehicle_type,
        COUNT(ac.id)::BIGINT                                AS total_calls,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ac.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS completed_calls,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ac.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS cancelled_calls,
        ROUND(AVG(
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (ac.arrived_at_scene - ac.dispatched_at)) / 60
        )::NUMERIC, 1)                                      AS avg_response_min,
        ROUND(AVG(
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (ac.completed_at - ac.dispatched_at)) / 60
        )::NUMERIC, 0)                                      AS avg_trip_duration,
        ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(ac.distance_km, 0))::NUMERIC, 1) AS total_distance_km,
        ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(ac.total_charge, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS total_revenue,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN ac.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF((p_to_date - p_from_date + 1) * 24, 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS utilization_pct
    FROM operations.ambulances am
    LEFT JOIN operations.ambulance_calls ac ON ac.ambulance_id = am.id
        AND ac.call_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    WHERE am.branch_id = p_branch_id AND am.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY am.vehicle_no, am.vehicle_type
    ORDER BY total_calls DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 23: WASTE MANAGEMENT REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_waste_management_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    waste_category      TEXT,
    is_hazardous        BOOLEAN,
    total_collections   BIGINT,
    total_weight_kg     NUMERIC,
    avg_weight_kg       NUMERIC,
    total_bags          BIGINT,
    disposal_method     TEXT,
    compliance_pct      NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        wc.name                                             AS waste_category,
        wc.is_hazardous,
        COUNT(wcl.id)::BIGINT                              AS total_collections,
        ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(wcl.weight_kg, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS total_weight_kg,
        ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(wcl.weight_kg, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS avg_weight_kg,
        SUM(COALESCE(wcl.bags_count, 0))::BIGINT           AS total_bags,
        wc.disposal_method,
        -- Compliance: ratio of collections with manifest vs total
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN wcl.manifest_no IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(wcl.id), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS compliance_pct
    FROM operations.waste_categories wc
    LEFT JOIN operations.waste_collection_logs wcl ON wcl.category_id = wc.id
        AND wcl.collection_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
        AND wcl.branch_id = p_branch_id
    WHERE wc.org_id IN (
        SELECT org_id FROM platform.branches WHERE id = p_branch_id
    )
    GROUP BY wc.name, wc.is_hazardous, wc.disposal_method
    ORDER BY total_weight_kg DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 24: VISITOR LOG REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_visitor_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    visit_date          DATE,
    total_visitors      BIGINT,
    currently_inside    BIGINT,
    avg_visit_duration  NUMERIC,
    ward_name           TEXT,
    peak_hour           INTEGER
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        vl.check_in::DATE                                   AS visit_date,
        COUNT(vl.id)::BIGINT                               AS total_visitors,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN vl.check_out IS NULL THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS currently_inside,
        ROUND(AVG(
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (COALESCE(vl.check_out, NOW()) - vl.check_in)) / 60
        )::NUMERIC, 0)                                      AS avg_visit_duration,
        w.name                                              AS ward_name,
        MODE() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY EXTRACT(HOUR FROM vl.check_in))::INTEGER AS peak_hour
    FROM operations.visitor_logs vl
    LEFT JOIN facility.wards w ON vl.ward_id = w.id
    WHERE vl.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND vl.check_in::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY vl.check_in::DATE, w.name
    ORDER BY visit_date DESC, total_visitors DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 25: COMPREHENSIVE NABH/JCI QUALITY INDICATORS
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_quality_indicators(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    indicator_code  TEXT,
    indicator_name  TEXT,
    category        TEXT,
    numerator       NUMERIC,
    denominator     NUMERIC,
    rate            NUMERIC,
    unit            TEXT,
    target          NUMERIC,
    status          TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY

    -- QI-1: Bed Occupancy Rate
    SELECT
        'QI-01'::TEXT, 'Bed Occupancy Rate'::TEXT, 'Operational'::TEXT,
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM facility.beds b
         JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
         JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND b.status = 'occupied'),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM facility.beds b
         JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
         JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND b.is_active = TRUE),
        ROUND((SELECT COUNT(*)::DECIMAL FROM facility.beds b
               JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
               JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
               WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND b.status = 'occupied') /
              NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*)::DECIMAL FROM facility.beds b2
                      JOIN facility.wards w2 ON b2.ward_id = w2.id
                      JOIN platform.branches br2 ON w2.branch_id = br2.id
                      WHERE br2.org_id = p_org_id AND b2.is_active = TRUE), 0) * 100, 1),
        '%'::TEXT, 80.0::NUMERIC, 'green'::TEXT

    UNION ALL

    -- QI-02: Average Length of Stay
    SELECT 'QI-02', 'Average Length of Stay', 'Clinical',
        ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1),
        COUNT(a.id)::NUMERIC,
        ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1),
        'Days', 4.0,
        CASE WHEN AVG(a.length_of_stay) <= 5 THEN 'green' ELSE 'yellow' END
    FROM clinical.admissions a
    JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND a.discharge_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- QI-03: Readmission Rate (within 30 days)
    SELECT 'QI-03', 'Readmission Rate (30 days)', 'Clinical',
        COUNT(CASE WHEN a2.id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END)::NUMERIC,
        COUNT(DISTINCT a.id)::NUMERIC,
        ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN a2.id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
              NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT a.id), 0) * 100, 2),
        '%', 5.0,
        CASE WHEN ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN a2.id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
                        NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT a.id), 0) * 100, 2) <= 5
             THEN 'green' ELSE 'red' END
    FROM clinical.admissions a
    JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a2 ON a2.patient_id = a.patient_id
        AND a2.id != a.id
        AND a2.admission_datetime BETWEEN a.discharge_datetime
            AND a.discharge_datetime + INTERVAL '30 days'
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND a.discharge_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- QI-04: Medication Error Rate
    SELECT 'QI-04', 'Medication Error Rate', 'Patient Safety',
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ma.status = 'missed' THEN 1 END)::NUMERIC,
        COUNT(ma.id)::NUMERIC,
        ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN ma.status = 'missed' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
              NULLIF(COUNT(ma.id), 0) * 100, 3),
        '%', 0.5,
        CASE WHEN ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN ma.status = 'missed' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
                        NULLIF(COUNT(ma.id), 0) * 100, 3) <= 0.5
             THEN 'green' ELSE 'red' END
    FROM clinical.medication_administrations ma
    JOIN clinical.medication_orders mo ON ma.order_id = mo.id
    JOIN clinical.admissions a ON mo.admission_id = a.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND ma.administered_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- QI-05: Patient Satisfaction Score
    SELECT 'QI-05', 'Patient Satisfaction Score', 'Quality',
        ROUND(AVG(pf.overall_rating)::NUMERIC, 2),
        COUNT(pf.id)::NUMERIC,
        ROUND(AVG(pf.overall_rating)::NUMERIC, 2),
        '/5', 4.0,
        CASE WHEN AVG(pf.overall_rating) >= 4 THEN 'green'
             WHEN AVG(pf.overall_rating) >= 3 THEN 'yellow'
             ELSE 'red' END
    FROM operations.patient_feedback pf
    JOIN platform.branches br ON pf.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND pf.submitted_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- QI-06: WHO Surgical Checklist Compliance
    SELECT 'QI-06', 'WHO Surgical Checklist Compliance', 'Patient Safety',
        COUNT(CASE WHEN s.who_sign_in_done AND s.who_time_out_done AND s.who_sign_out_done THEN 1 END)::NUMERIC,
        COUNT(s.id)::NUMERIC,
        ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN s.who_sign_in_done AND s.who_time_out_done AND s.who_sign_out_done THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
              NULLIF(COUNT(s.id), 0) * 100, 1),
        '%', 100.0,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(CASE WHEN NOT (s.who_sign_in_done AND s.who_time_out_done AND s.who_sign_out_done) THEN 1 END) = 0
             THEN 'green' ELSE 'red' END
    FROM clinical.surgeries s
    JOIN platform.branches br ON s.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND s.status = 'completed'

    UNION ALL

    -- QI-07: Lab TAT Compliance
    SELECT 'QI-07', 'Lab TAT Compliance Rate', 'Operational',
        COUNT(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600 <= tm.tat_hours THEN 1 END)::NUMERIC,
        COUNT(lr.id)::NUMERIC,
        ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600 <= tm.tat_hours THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
              NULLIF(COUNT(lr.id), 0) * 100, 1),
        '%', 95.0,
        CASE WHEN ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600 <= tm.tat_hours THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
                        NULLIF(COUNT(lr.id), 0) * 100, 1) >= 95
             THEN 'green' ELSE 'yellow' END
    FROM clinical.lab_results lr
    JOIN clinical.lab_order_items loi ON lr.order_item_id = loi.id
    JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON loi.order_id = lo.id
    JOIN clinical.lab_samples ls ON lr.sample_id = ls.id
    JOIN clinical.test_master tm ON lr.test_id = tm.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON lo.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND lr.entered_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND ls.collected_at IS NOT NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```
