## 🔵 MISSING COLUMNS FIX & CROSS-REFERENCE

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- ADD MISSING COLUMNS (Cross-module references)
-- ============================================================

-- Add visit branch_id (referenced in views)
ALTER TABLE clinical.patient_visits
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS branch_id UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id);

-- Add visit_id to vital signs (referenced in views)
ALTER TABLE clinical.vital_signs
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS visit_id UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id);

-- Add high_alert to medication orders (referenced in views)
ALTER TABLE clinical.medication_orders
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_high_alert BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;

-- Add branch_id to clinical.lab_orders (ensure exists)
ALTER TABLE clinical.lab_orders
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS branch_id UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id);

-- Add extended columns to clinical.admissions
ALTER TABLE clinical.admissions
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS discharge_plan TEXT,
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS expected_discharge_date DATE,
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS mlc_case BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS mlc_no VARCHAR(50);

-- Add telemedicine session reference to bills
ALTER TABLE finance.bills
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS telemedicine_id UUID REFERENCES clinical.telemedicine_sessions(id);

-- Add doctor signature to prescriptions (already has digital_signature)
-- Add prescription_type enum check
ALTER TABLE pharmacy.prescriptions
    ADD CONSTRAINT chk_rx_type CHECK (
        prescription_type IN ('opd', 'ipd', 'discharge', 'emergency', 'refill', 'telemedicine')
    );

-- ============================================================
-- SEQUENCES FOR AUTO-NUMBERS
-- ============================================================

CREATE SEQUENCE finance.bill_no_seq    START 10001;
CREATE SEQUENCE finance.payment_no_seq START 20001;
CREATE SEQUENCE clinical.lab_order_seq START 30001;
CREATE SEQUENCE clinical.admit_no_seq  START 40001;
CREATE SEQUENCE operations.po_no_seq   START 50001;
CREATE SEQUENCE operations.grn_no_seq  START 60001;
CREATE SEQUENCE operations.call_no_seq START 70001;

-- Auto Bill Number
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.generate_bill_no()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.bill_no IS NULL THEN
        NEW.bill_no := 'BILL' || LPAD(nextval('finance.bill_no_seq')::TEXT, 7, '0');
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_bill_no
BEFORE INSERT ON finance.bills
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.generate_bill_no();

-- Auto Admission Number
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.generate_admission_no()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.admission_no IS NULL THEN
        NEW.admission_no := 'IPD' || LPAD(nextval('clinical.admit_no_seq')::TEXT, 7, '0');
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_admission_no
BEFORE INSERT ON clinical.admissions
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.generate_admission_no();

-- Auto Lab Order Number
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.generate_lab_order_no()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.order_no IS NULL THEN
        NEW.order_no := 'LAB' || LPAD(nextval('clinical.lab_order_seq')::TEXT, 7, '0');
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_lab_order_no
BEFORE INSERT ON clinical.lab_orders
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.generate_lab_order_no();

-- Auto PO Number
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.generate_po_no()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.po_no IS NULL THEN
        NEW.po_no := 'PO' || LPAD(nextval('operations.po_no_seq')::TEXT, 7, '0');
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_po_no
BEFORE INSERT ON operations.purchase_orders
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.generate_po_no();
```

## 📊 COMPLETE INDEX STRATEGY (Extended)

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- EXTENDED PERFORMANCE INDEXES
-- ============================================================

-- Clinical
CREATE INDEX idx_vital_patient_date    ON clinical.vital_signs(patient_id, recorded_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_critical  ON clinical.lab_results(is_critical, critical_notified_at)
    WHERE is_critical = TRUE AND critical_notified_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_status    ON clinical.lab_results(status) WHERE status = 'draft';
CREATE INDEX idx_med_orders_active     ON clinical.medication_orders(patient_id, status)
    WHERE status = 'active';
CREATE INDEX idx_med_admin_date        ON clinical.medication_administrations(administered_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_date        ON clinical.surgeries(scheduled_datetime, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_date     ON clinical.appointments(appointment_datetime, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_appt_doctor_date      ON clinical.appointments(doctor_id, appointment_datetime);

-- Facility
CREATE INDEX idx_beds_status           ON facility.beds(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_beds_ward             ON facility.beds(ward_id, status);

-- Finance
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_balance         ON finance.bills(balance_amount) WHERE balance_amount > 0;
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_org_date        ON finance.bills(org_id, bill_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_payments_date         ON finance.payments(payment_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_claims_status         ON finance.insurance_claims(status, submitted_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_journal_date          ON finance.journal_entries(entry_date, is_posted);
CREATE INDEX idx_assets_status         ON finance.fixed_assets(status, branch_id);

-- Pharmacy
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_expiry_low      ON pharmacy.medicine_stock(expiry_date, quantity)
    WHERE quantity > 0;
CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_date       ON pharmacy.dispensings(dispensed_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_patient  ON pharmacy.prescriptions(patient_id, prescribed_at);

-- Operations
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_ledger_store    ON operations.stock_ledger(store_id, item_id, transaction_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_po_supplier           ON operations.purchase_orders(supplier_id, po_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_grn_po                ON operations.goods_receipt_notes(po_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_indent_dept           ON operations.department_indents(department_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_complaints_org        ON operations.complaints(org_id, status, created_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_feedback_branch       ON operations.patient_feedback(branch_id, submitted_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_visitor_branch_date   ON operations.visitor_logs(branch_id, check_in);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_inventory       ON operations.blood_bank_inventory(blood_group, component, status)
    WHERE status = 'available';
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_status      ON operations.ambulance_calls(status, branch_id);

-- HR
CREATE INDEX idx_attendance_month      ON hr.attendance(employee_id, attendance_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_payslips_run          ON hr.payslips(payroll_run_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_leave_emp_year        ON hr.leave_balances(employee_id, year);

-- Audit
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_created         ON audit.audit_logs(created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_notif_unsent          ON audit.notifications_queue(is_sent, created_at)
    WHERE is_sent = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_notif_log_status      ON audit.notification_logs(status, created_at)
    WHERE status = 'pending';

```
-- ============================================================
-- FACILITY: MAINTENANCE MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE facility.equipment_registry (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    equipment_name      VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    equipment_code      VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE,
    category            VARCHAR(50),    -- medical/non_medical/it/hvac/electrical
    model_no            VARCHAR(100),
    serial_no           VARCHAR(100),
    manufacturer        VARCHAR(200),
    vendor_id           UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    purchase_date       DATE,
    purchase_price      DECIMAL(14,2),
    warranty_expiry     DATE,
    amc_start_date      DATE,
    amc_end_date        DATE,
    amc_vendor_id       UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    amc_amount          DECIMAL(12,2),
    location_ward_id    UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    location_room       VARCHAR(100),
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active', -- active/under_maintenance/condemned
    manual_url          TEXT,
    photo_url           TEXT,
    last_calibration    DATE,
    next_calibration    DATE,
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE facility.maintenance_schedules (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    equipment_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    schedule_type       VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- daily/weekly/monthly/quarterly/annual
    task_description    TEXT NOT NULL,
    checklist           JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    assigned_to         UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    vendor_id           UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    frequency_days      INTEGER,
    last_done_date      DATE,
    next_due_date       DATE,
    estimated_hours     DECIMAL(5,2),
    estimated_cost      DECIMAL(12,2),
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE facility.maintenance_work_orders (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    equipment_id        UUID REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    schedule_id         UUID REFERENCES facility.maintenance_schedules(id),
    wo_no               VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    wo_type             VARCHAR(30),    -- preventive/breakdown/calibration/civil/electrical
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal', -- critical/high/normal/low
    title               VARCHAR(300),
    description         TEXT,
    reported_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    reported_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    assigned_to         UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    vendor_id           UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    
    -- Timing
    scheduled_date      DATE,
    started_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    downtime_hours      DECIMAL(8,2),
    
    -- Resolution
    root_cause          TEXT,
    action_taken        TEXT,
    parts_used          JSONB DEFAULT '[]',  -- [{name, qty, cost}]
    labor_cost          DECIMAL(12,2),
    parts_cost          DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_cost          DECIMAL(12,2),
    
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'open', -- open/assigned/in_progress/completed/cancelled
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-compute total cost trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.compute_wo_total_cost()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.total_cost := COALESCE(NEW.labor_cost, 0) + COALESCE(NEW.parts_cost, 0);
    IF NEW.completed_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.started_at IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.downtime_hours := ROUND(
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NEW.completed_at - NEW.started_at)) / 3600, 2
        );
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_wo_total_cost
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON facility.maintenance_work_orders
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.compute_wo_total_cost();

CREATE TABLE facility.calibration_records (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    equipment_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    calibration_date    DATE NOT NULL,
    next_due_date       DATE,
    performed_by        VARCHAR(200),   -- internal/external agency
    agency_name         VARCHAR(200),
    certificate_no      VARCHAR(100),
    certificate_url     TEXT,
    result              VARCHAR(30),    -- pass/fail/conditional
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-alert for calibration due
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.alert_calibration_due()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.next_due_date IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE facility.equipment_registry
        SET next_calibration = NEW.next_due_date,
            last_calibration = NEW.calibration_date
        WHERE id = NEW.equipment_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_calibration_update
AFTER INSERT ON facility.calibration_records
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.alert_calibration_due();

CREATE TABLE facility.spare_parts_inventory (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    part_name       VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    part_code       VARCHAR(50),
    equipment_id    UUID REFERENCES facility.equipment_registry(id),
    quantity        INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    min_quantity    INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    unit_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),
    supplier_id     UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    location        VARCHAR(100),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- FACILITY: BUILDING MANAGEMENT (Extended)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE facility.utility_meters (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    meter_type      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- electricity/water/gas/generator
    meter_no        VARCHAR(100),
    location        VARCHAR(200),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE facility.utility_readings (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    meter_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.utility_meters(id),
    reading_date    DATE NOT NULL,
    reading_value   DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
    unit            VARCHAR(20),    -- kWh/liter/cubic_meter
    consumption     DECIMAL(12,2),  -- computed from prev reading
    unit_rate       DECIMAL(10,4),
    total_cost      DECIMAL(12,2),
    read_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-compute consumption trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.compute_utility_consumption()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_prev_reading DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    SELECT reading_value INTO v_prev_reading
    FROM facility.utility_readings
    WHERE meter_id = NEW.meter_id AND reading_date < NEW.reading_date
    ORDER BY reading_date DESC
    LIMIT 1;

    IF v_prev_reading IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.consumption := NEW.reading_value - v_prev_reading;
        NEW.total_cost  := NEW.consumption * COALESCE(NEW.unit_rate, 0);
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_utility_consumption
BEFORE INSERT ON facility.utility_readings
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.compute_utility_consumption();

CREATE TABLE facility.security_incidents (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    incident_type   VARCHAR(50),    -- theft/fire/violence/accident/intrusion
    incident_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    location        VARCHAR(200),
    description     TEXT,
    reported_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    assigned_to     UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'open',
    resolution      TEXT,
    resolved_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

-- ============================================================
-- OPERATIONS: SUPPLY CHAIN & INVENTORY (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.item_categories (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),

    parent_id   UUID REFERENCES operations.item_categories(id),
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.item_master (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES operations.item_categories(id),
    item_code           VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    item_name           VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description         TEXT,
    unit_of_measure     VARCHAR(30),    -- piece/box/liter/kg/meter
    reorder_level       INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    min_stock           INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    max_stock           INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    standard_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),
    is_expirable        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_serialized       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,

);

CREATE TABLE operations.stores (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    store_type      VARCHAR(30),    -- central/sub_store/ward_store/pharmacy
    floor_id        UUID REFERENCES facility.floors(id),
    in_charge_id    UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- PROCUREMENT CYCLE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_requisitions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    pr_no               VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    requested_by        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    department_id       UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id),
    required_date       DATE,
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft/submitted/approved/po_raised/rejected
    approved_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    rejection_reason    TEXT,
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_requisition_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    pr_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.purchase_requisitions(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    quantity        DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    unit            VARCHAR(30),
    estimated_cost  DECIMAL(12,2),
    remarks         TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE operations.request_for_quotations (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    pr_id           UUID REFERENCES operations.purchase_requisitions(id),
    rfq_no          VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    issued_date     DATE NOT NULL,
    closing_date    DATE,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'open',

    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.rfq_suppliers (
    rfq_id          UUID REFERENCES operations.request_for_quotations(id),
    supplier_id     UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),

CREATE TABLE operations.quotations (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    rfq_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.request_for_quotations(id),

    quotation_date  DATE,
    valid_until     DATE,
    total_amount    DECIMAL(14,2),
    delivery_days   INTEGER,
    payment_terms   VARCHAR(100),

    remarks         TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.quotation_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    quotation_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.quotations(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),

    unit_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_price     DECIMAL(12,2),

    remarks         TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_orders (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    pr_id               UUID REFERENCES operations.purchase_requisitions(id),
    quotation_id        UUID REFERENCES operations.quotations(id),
    po_no               VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    po_date             DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    supplier_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    delivery_date       DATE,
    payment_terms       VARCHAR(100),
    delivery_address    TEXT,
    gross_amount        DECIMAL(14,2),
    discount_amount     DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount          DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount          DECIMAL(14,2),
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft/approved/sent/partial/received/closed/cancelled
    approved_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.purchase_order_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    po_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.purchase_orders(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    ordered_qty     DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    received_qty    DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    pending_qty     DECIMAL(10,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (ordered_qty - received_qty) STORED,
    unit_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    discount_pct    DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_pct         DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_price       DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_amount    DECIMAL(14,2),
    remarks         TEXT
);

-- ============================================================
-- GOODS RECEIPT & STOCK
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.goods_receipt_notes (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    po_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.purchase_orders(id),
    store_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.stores(id),
    grn_no          VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    grn_date        DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    supplier_id     UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    invoice_no      VARCHAR(100),
    invoice_date    DATE,
    invoice_amount  DECIMAL(14,2),
    qc_status       VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending/passed/failed/partial
    qc_by           UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    received_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft/posted/cancelled
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.grn_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    grn_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.goods_receipt_notes(id),
    po_item_id      UUID REFERENCES operations.purchase_order_items(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    ordered_qty     DECIMAL(10,2),
    received_qty    DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    accepted_qty    DECIMAL(10,2),
    rejected_qty    DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    rejection_reason VARCHAR(200),
    batch_no        VARCHAR(100),
    expiry_date     DATE,
    unit_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_price     DECIMAL(14,2)
);

CREATE TABLE operations.stock_ledger (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    store_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.stores(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    transaction_type VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- grn/issue/return/transfer_in/transfer_out/adjustment/opening
    reference_type  VARCHAR(50),           -- grn/indent/transfer/adjustment
    reference_id    UUID,
    batch_no        VARCHAR(100),
    expiry_date     DATE,
    transaction_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    in_qty          DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    out_qty         DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    balance_qty     DECIMAL(10,2),
    unit_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_value     DECIMAL(14,2),
    narration       TEXT,
    created_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Current stock balance materialized view
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW operations.mv_current_stock AS
SELECT
    sl.store_id,
    st.name AS store_name,
    st.branch_id,
    sl.item_id,
    im.item_name,
    im.item_code,
    im.unit_of_measure,
    SUM(sl.in_qty - sl.out_qty) AS current_qty,
    SUM(sl.total_value) AS stock_value,
    im.reorder_level,
    im.min_stock,
    CASE
        WHEN SUM(sl.in_qty - sl.out_qty) <= im.min_stock    THEN 'critical'
        WHEN SUM(sl.in_qty - sl.out_qty) <= im.reorder_level THEN 'low'
        ELSE 'normal'
    END AS stock_status
FROM operations.stock_ledger sl
JOIN operations.stores st ON sl.store_id = st.id
JOIN operations.item_master im ON sl.item_id = im.id
GROUP BY sl.store_id, st.name, st.branch_id, sl.item_id, im.item_name, im.item_code,
         im.unit_of_measure, im.reorder_level, im.min_stock;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON operations.mv_current_stock(store_id, item_id);

-- Refresh stock view on ledger insert
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.refresh_stock_view()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY operations.mv_current_stock;
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_refresh_stock
AFTER INSERT ON operations.stock_ledger
FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.refresh_stock_view();

-- ============================================================
-- DEPARTMENT INDENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.department_indents (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    department_id   UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id),
    from_store_id   UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.stores(id),
    indent_no       VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    indent_date     DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    required_date   DATE,
    requested_by    UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    issued_by       UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending/approved/issued/rejected
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.indent_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    indent_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.department_indents(id),
    item_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.item_master(id),
    requested_qty   DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    approved_qty    DECIMAL(10,2),
    issued_qty      DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    unit_cost       DECIMAL(12,2),
    remarks         TEXT
);

-- Post stock ledger on indent issue
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.post_indent_to_ledger()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'issued' AND OLD.status != 'issued' THEN
        INSERT INTO operations.stock_ledger(
            store_id, item_id, transaction_type, reference_type,
            reference_id, transaction_date, out_qty, narration, created_at
        )
        SELECT
            NEW.from_store_id,
            ii.item_id,
            'issue',
            'indent',
            NEW.id,
            CURRENT_DATE,
            COALESCE(ii.issued_qty, 0),
            'Issue against Indent: ' || NEW.indent_no,
            NOW()
        FROM operations.indent_items ii
        WHERE ii.indent_id = NEW.id AND COALESCE(ii.issued_qty, 0) > 0;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_indent_post_ledger
AFTER UPDATE ON operations.department_indents
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.post_indent_to_ledger();

-- Post stock ledger on GRN
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.post_grn_to_ledger()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'posted' AND OLD.status != 'posted' THEN
        INSERT INTO operations.stock_ledger(
            store_id, item_id, transaction_type, reference_type,
            reference_id, batch_no, expiry_date, transaction_date,
            in_qty, unit_cost, total_value, narration, created_at
        )
        SELECT
            NEW.store_id,
            gi.item_id,
            'grn',
            'grn',
            NEW.id,
            gi.batch_no,
            gi.expiry_date,
            NEW.grn_date,
            COALESCE(gi.accepted_qty, gi.received_qty),
            gi.unit_price,
            COALESCE(gi.accepted_qty, gi.received_qty) * COALESCE(gi.unit_price, 0),
            'GRN: ' || NEW.grn_no,
            NOW()
        FROM operations.grn_items gi
        WHERE gi.grn_id = NEW.id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_grn_post_ledger
AFTER UPDATE ON operations.goods_receipt_notes
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.post_grn_to_ledger();
```

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: CONSENT FORM MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.consent_templates (

    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    consent_type    VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                    -- general/surgical/anesthesia/blood_transfusion/procedure/
                    -- telemedicine/research/photography/data_sharing/dnar
    title           VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    title_bn        VARCHAR(300),
    content_en      TEXT NOT NULL,
    content_bn      TEXT,

    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_consents (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    surgery_id          UUID REFERENCES clinical.surgeries(id),
    template_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.consent_templates(id),
    consent_type        VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Consent process
    explained_by        UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),   -- doctor
    explained_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    language_used       VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'bn',
    interpreter_used    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    interpreter_name    VARCHAR(200),
    
    -- Consent given
    consented_by        VARCHAR(200),                         -- patient/guardian name
    relation_to_patient VARCHAR(50),                          -- self/guardian/spouse
    signed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    patient_signature   TEXT,                                 -- base64 / URL
    witness_signature   TEXT,
    witness_name        VARCHAR(200),
    
    -- Status
    is_withdrawn        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    withdrawn_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    withdrawn_by        UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    withdrawal_reason   TEXT,
    
    -- Storage
    pdf_url             TEXT,
    is_verified         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Alert if consent missing before surgery
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.check_consent_before_surgery()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_consent_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'in_progress' THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_consent_count
        FROM clinical.patient_consents
        WHERE surgery_id = NEW.id
          AND consent_type IN ('surgical', 'anesthesia')
          AND is_withdrawn = FALSE;

        IF v_consent_count < 2 THEN
            RAISE WARNING 'Surgery % starting without required consents!', NEW.id;
            INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
                notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
                message, priority, created_at
            ) VALUES (
                'missing_consent', NEW.id, 'clinical.surgeries',
                'WARNING: Surgery starting without signed consents!',
                'urgent', NOW()
            );
        END IF;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_consent_check
BEFORE UPDATE ON clinical.surgeries
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.check_consent_before_surgery();
```

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- OPERATIONS: COMPLAINT & FEEDBACK
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.complaint_categories (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- staff/treatment/billing/cleanliness/food/wait_time/pharmacy/lab
    sla_hours   INTEGER DEFAULT 24,
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.complaints (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    complaint_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Complainant
    patient_id          UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    complainant_name    VARCHAR(200),
    complainant_phone   VARCHAR(20),
    complainant_email   VARCHAR(150),
    complainant_type    VARCHAR(30),    -- patient/attendant/visitor/staff/anonymous
    
    -- Complaint details
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES operations.complaint_categories(id),
    channel             VARCHAR(30),    -- walk_in/phone/online/app/email
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    subject             VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description         TEXT,
    incident_date       DATE,
    department_id       UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id),
    against_staff_id    UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    
    -- Processing
    assigned_to         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    assigned_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    investigation_notes TEXT,
    root_cause          TEXT,
    corrective_action   TEXT,
    
    -- Resolution
    resolved_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    resolved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolution_notes    TEXT,
    sla_breach          BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Feedback
    complainant_satisfied BOOLEAN,
    reopen_count        INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'open',
                        -- open/acknowledged/investigating/resolved/closed/escalated
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- SLA breach check trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.check_complaint_sla()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_sla_hours INTEGER;
BEGIN
    SELECT sla_hours INTO v_sla_hours
    FROM operations.complaint_categories
    WHERE id = NEW.category_id;

    IF NEW.status NOT IN ('resolved', 'closed') THEN
        IF NOW() > (NEW.created_at + (v_sla_hours || ' hours')::INTERVAL) THEN
            NEW.sla_breach := TRUE;
            INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
                notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
                message, priority, created_at
            ) VALUES (
                'sla_breach', NEW.id, 'operations.complaints',
                'SLA breached for complaint: ' || NEW.complaint_no,
                'high', NOW()
            );
        END IF;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_complaint_sla
BEFORE UPDATE ON operations.complaints
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.check_complaint_sla();

CREATE TABLE operations.complaint_timeline (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    complaint_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.complaints(id),
    action          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    notes           TEXT,
    done_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    done_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- FEEDBACK & PATIENT SATISFACTION
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE operations.feedback_forms (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    form_type       VARCHAR(30),    -- discharge/opd/telemedicine/general
    title           VARCHAR(200),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.feedback_questions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    form_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.feedback_forms(id),
    question_text   VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    question_type   VARCHAR(30),    -- rating/yes_no/text/nps
    category        VARCHAR(50),    -- doctor/nurse/facility/food/cleanliness/billing
    sort_order      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_required     BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.patient_feedback (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    form_id         UUID REFERENCES operations.feedback_forms(id),
    patient_id      UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    channel         VARCHAR(30),    -- kiosk/online/whatsapp/paper/qr
    nps_score       INTEGER,        -- 0-10 Net Promoter Score
    overall_rating  DECIMAL(3,1),   -- 1-5
    submitted_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    is_anonymous    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.feedback_responses (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    feedback_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.patient_feedback(id),
    question_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.feedback_questions(id),
    rating_value    INTEGER,        -- for rating questions (1-5)
    text_value      TEXT,           -- for text questions
    boolean_value   BOOLEAN         -- for yes/no questions
);

-- Patient Satisfaction Score View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_patient_satisfaction AS
SELECT
    pf.branch_id,
    b.name AS branch_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at) AS month,
    pf.form_id,
    COUNT(pf.id) AS total_responses,
    ROUND(AVG(pf.overall_rating)::NUMERIC, 2) AS avg_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(pf.nps_score)::NUMERIC, 1) AS avg_nps,
    -- NPS = % promoters (9-10) - % detractors (0-6)
    ROUND(
        (COUNT(CASE WHEN pf.nps_score >= 9 THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
         NULLIF(COUNT(pf.id), 0) * 100) -
        (COUNT(CASE WHEN pf.nps_score <= 6 THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
         NULLIF(COUNT(pf.id), 0) * 100), 1
    ) AS nps_score,
    -- Category ratings
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'doctor'      THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS doctor_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'nurse'       THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS nurse_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'cleanliness' THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS cleanliness_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'food'        THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS food_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'billing'     THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS billing_rating,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'facility'    THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS facility_rating
FROM operations.patient_feedback pf
JOIN platform.branches b ON pf.branch_id = b.id
LEFT JOIN operations.feedback_responses fr ON fr.feedback_id = pf.id
LEFT JOIN operations.feedback_questions fq ON fr.question_id = fq.id
GROUP BY pf.branch_id, b.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at), pf.form_id;
```

    -- Session

    platform            VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'jitsi',   -- jitsi/zoom/custom
    room_name           VARCHAR(200),
    meeting_url         TEXT,

    -- Timing
    scheduled_datetime  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    duration_minutes    INTEGER DEFAULT 15,
    actual_start        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    actual_end          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    actual_duration     INTEGER,
    
    -- Patient inputs
    chief_complaint     TEXT,
    self_reported_vitals JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
                        -- {bp, pulse, temp, spo2, weight}
    
    -- Clinical outputs
    prescription_id     UUID REFERENCES pharmacy.prescriptions(id),
    lab_order_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.lab_orders(id),
    follow_up_date      DATE,
    follow_up_notes     TEXT,
    
    -- Technical
    call_quality        VARCHAR(20),    -- good/fair/poor
    technical_issues    TEXT,
    
    -- Recording
    is_recorded         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    recording_url       TEXT,
    recording_consent   BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    recording_expiry    DATE,
    
    -- Billing
    fee_amount          DECIMAL(12,2),
    bill_id             UUID REFERENCES finance.bills(id),
    payment_status      VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    -- Status
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'scheduled',
                        -- scheduled/waiting/in_progress/completed/cancelled/no_show
    
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- DOCTOR APPOINTMENT — COMPLETE MODULE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.appointment_slots (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    doctor_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    slot_date       DATE NOT NULL,
    start_time      TIME NOT NULL,
    end_time        TIME NOT NULL,
    slot_type       VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'opd',  -- opd/telemedicine
    max_patients    INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    booked_count    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'available', -- available/full/blocked/holiday
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(doctor_id, slot_date, start_time, slot_type)
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.appointments (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    doctor_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    slot_id             UUID REFERENCES clinical.appointment_slots(id),
    appointment_no      VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    appointment_type    VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'opd',
    appointment_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    token_no            INTEGER,
    chief_complaint     TEXT,
    visit_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    booked_by           UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    booking_channel     VARCHAR(30), -- walk_in/phone/online/app
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'booked',
                        -- booked/confirmed/arrived/in_consultation/completed/cancelled/no_show
    arrived_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_start  TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_end    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    wait_time_minutes   INTEGER,
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Update slot booked count
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.update_slot_count()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' AND NEW.slot_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE clinical.appointment_slots
        SET booked_count = booked_count + 1,
            status = CASE
                WHEN (booked_count + 1) >= max_patients THEN 'full'
                ELSE status
            END
        WHERE id = NEW.slot_id;
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.status = 'cancelled'
          AND OLD.status != 'cancelled' AND NEW.slot_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE clinical.appointment_slots
        SET booked_count = GREATEST(booked_count - 1, 0),
            status = CASE
                WHEN status = 'full' THEN 'available'
                ELSE status
            END
        WHERE id = NEW.slot_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_slot_count
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON clinical.appointments
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.update_slot_count();
```

## 🔵 ACCOUNTS & FINANCE — COMPLETE

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: CHART OF ACCOUNTS (COA)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.account_groups (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    type        VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,   -- asset/liability/equity/income/expense
    parent_id   UUID REFERENCES finance.account_groups(id),
    sort_order  INTEGER DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE TABLE finance.chart_of_accounts (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    account_group_id UUID REFERENCES finance.account_groups(id),
    account_code    VARCHAR(20) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    account_name    VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    account_type    VARCHAR(30),    -- asset/liability/equity/income/expense
    is_bank_account BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_cash_account BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_control_account BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    parent_id       UUID REFERENCES finance.chart_of_accounts(id),
    balance         DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0,    -- running balance
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.journal_entries (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    entry_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    entry_date      DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    entry_type      VARCHAR(30),    -- payment/receipt/journal/contra
    reference_type  VARCHAR(50),    -- bill/payment/payroll/purchase
    reference_id    UUID,
    description     TEXT,
    total_debit     DECIMAL(16,2),
    total_credit    DECIMAL(16,2),
    is_posted       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    posted_by       UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.journal_entry_lines (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    entry_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.journal_entries(id),
    account_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.chart_of_accounts(id),
    description     VARCHAR(300),
    debit_amount    DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0,
    credit_amount   DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0,
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    cost_center_id  UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id)
);

-- Validate journal entry balanced trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.validate_journal_entry()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_total_debit  DECIMAL;
    v_total_credit DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    SELECT
        COALESCE(SUM(debit_amount), 0),
        COALESCE(SUM(credit_amount), 0)
    INTO v_total_debit, v_total_credit
    FROM finance.journal_entry_lines
    WHERE entry_id = NEW.entry_id;

    IF ABS(v_total_debit - v_total_credit) > 0.01 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Journal entry % is not balanced. Debit: %, Credit: %',
            NEW.entry_id, v_total_debit, v_total_credit;
    END IF;

    UPDATE finance.journal_entries
    SET total_debit = v_total_debit, total_credit = v_total_credit
    WHERE id = NEW.entry_id;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_journal_balance
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON finance.journal_entry_lines
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.validate_journal_entry();

-- Update account balance on journal post
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.update_account_balance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.is_posted = TRUE AND OLD.is_posted = FALSE THEN
        UPDATE finance.chart_of_accounts coa
        SET balance = balance + jel.debit_amount - jel.credit_amount
        FROM finance.journal_entry_lines jel
        WHERE jel.entry_id = NEW.id AND jel.account_id = coa.id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_account_balance
AFTER UPDATE ON finance.journal_entries
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.update_account_balance();

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: ASSET MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.asset_categories (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    depreciation_method VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'straight_line',
    useful_life_years INTEGER,
    salvage_pct     DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE finance.fixed_assets (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES finance.asset_categories(id),
    asset_code          VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    asset_name          VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    purchase_date       DATE,
    purchase_price      DECIMAL(14,2),
    salvage_value       DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    useful_life_years   INTEGER,
    depreciation_method VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'straight_line',
    accumulated_depreciation DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    book_value          DECIMAL(14,2),
    location_id         UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    condition           VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'good',
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active',
    disposal_date       DATE,
    disposal_value      DECIMAL(14,2),
    disposal_reason     TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-compute book value
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.compute_asset_book_value()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.book_value := NEW.purchase_price - COALESCE(NEW.accumulated_depreciation, 0);
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_asset_book_value
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON finance.fixed_assets
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.compute_asset_book_value();

CREATE TABLE finance.asset_depreciations (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    asset_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.fixed_assets(id),
    fiscal_year     INTEGER NOT NULL,
    period_month    INTEGER,
    depreciation    DECIMAL(14,2),
    accumulated     DECIMAL(14,2),
    book_value      DECIMAL(14,2),
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(asset_id, fiscal_year, period_month)
);
```

## 🔵 ADDITIONAL STORED PROCEDURES

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- SP 11: SUPPLY CHAIN PROCUREMENT REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_procurement_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    po_no           TEXT,
    po_date         DATE,
    supplier_name   TEXT,
    total_items     BIGINT,
    po_amount       NUMERIC,
    received_value  NUMERIC,
    pending_value   NUMERIC,
    po_status       TEXT,
    grn_count       BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        po.po_no,
        po.po_date,
        s.name                                              AS supplier_name,
        COUNT(poi.id)::BIGINT                              AS total_items,
        ROUND(po.net_amount::NUMERIC, 2)                   AS po_amount,
        ROUND(SUM(poi.received_qty * poi.unit_price)::NUMERIC, 2) AS received_value,
        ROUND(SUM(poi.pending_qty * poi.unit_price)::NUMERIC, 2)  AS pending_value,
        po.status                                           AS po_status,
        COUNT(DISTINCT grn.id)::BIGINT                     AS grn_count
    FROM operations.purchase_orders po
    JOIN finance.suppliers s ON po.supplier_id = s.id
    JOIN operations.purchase_order_items poi ON poi.po_id = po.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.goods_receipt_notes grn ON grn.po_id = po.id
    WHERE po.branch_id IN (
        SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id
    )
    AND po.po_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY po.po_no, po.po_date, s.name, po.net_amount, po.status
    ORDER BY po.po_date DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 12: STOCK CONSUMPTION ANALYSIS
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_stock_consumption_report(
    p_store_id      UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    item_code       TEXT,
    item_name       TEXT,
    category        TEXT,
    opening_qty     NUMERIC,
    received_qty    NUMERIC,
    issued_qty      NUMERIC,
    closing_qty     NUMERIC,
    consumption_value NUMERIC,
    abc_class       TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH consumption AS (
        SELECT
            sl.item_id,
            SUM(CASE WHEN sl.transaction_type = 'grn' THEN sl.in_qty ELSE 0 END) AS received,
            SUM(CASE WHEN sl.transaction_type IN ('issue', 'transfer_out') THEN sl.out_qty ELSE 0 END) AS issued,
            SUM(sl.in_qty * COALESCE(sl.unit_cost, 0) -
                sl.out_qty * COALESCE(sl.unit_cost, 0)) AS value
        FROM operations.stock_ledger sl
        WHERE sl.store_id = p_store_id
          AND sl.transaction_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
        GROUP BY sl.item_id
    ),
    ranked AS (
        SELECT *,
               NTILE(3) OVER (ORDER BY value DESC) AS tile
        FROM consumption
    )
    SELECT
        im.item_code,
        im.item_name,
        ic.name                                             AS category,
        COALESCE(cs.current_qty + r.issued - r.received, 0)::NUMERIC AS opening_qty,
        ROUND(r.received::NUMERIC, 2)                       AS received_qty,
        ROUND(r.issued::NUMERIC, 2)                         AS issued_qty,
        ROUND(cs.current_qty::NUMERIC, 2)                   AS closing_qty,
        ROUND(r.value::NUMERIC, 2)                          AS consumption_value,
        CASE r.tile WHEN 1 THEN 'A' WHEN 2 THEN 'B' ELSE 'C' END AS abc_class
    FROM ranked r
    JOIN operations.item_master im ON r.item_id = im.id
    JOIN operations.item_categories ic ON im.category_id = ic.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.mv_current_stock cs ON cs.item_id = r.item_id AND cs.store_id = p_store_id
    ORDER BY r.value DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 13: HR ATTENDANCE SUMMARY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_attendance_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_month         INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_year          INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_department_id UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    employee_code   TEXT,
    employee_name   TEXT,
    department      TEXT,
    designation     TEXT,
    total_days      INTEGER,
    present_days    BIGINT,
    absent_days     BIGINT,
    late_days       BIGINT,
    leave_days      NUMERIC,
    overtime_hours  NUMERIC,
    attendance_pct  NUMERIC
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_total_days INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Get working days in month
    v_total_days := 26;

    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        e.employee_code,
        e.full_name                                         AS employee_name,
        dep.name                                            AS department,
        des.name                                            AS designation,
        v_total_days                                        AS total_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'present' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS present_days,
        (v_total_days - COUNT(att.id))::BIGINT              AS absent_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'late' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS late_days,
        COALESCE(SUM(la.days), 0)::NUMERIC                  AS leave_days,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(att.overtime_hours), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS overtime_hours,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'present' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            v_total_days * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS attendance_pct
    FROM hr.employees e
    JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
    JOIN hr.designations des ON e.designation_id = des.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.attendance att ON att.employee_id = e.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM att.attendance_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM att.attendance_date) = p_year
    LEFT JOIN hr.leave_applications la ON la.employee_id = e.id
        AND la.status = 'approved'
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM la.from_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM la.from_date) = p_year
    WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id
      AND e.status = 'active'
      AND (p_department_id IS NULL OR e.department_id = p_department_id)
    GROUP BY e.employee_code, e.full_name, dep.name, des.name
    ORDER BY dep.name, e.full_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 14: MAINTENANCE EQUIPMENT STATUS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_equipment_status_report(
    p_branch_id     UUID,
    p_include_stats BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    equipment_name  TEXT,
    category        TEXT,
    serial_no       TEXT,
    location        TEXT,
    status          TEXT,
    last_maintained DATE,
    next_pm_due     DATE,
    calibration_due DATE,
    warranty_expiry DATE,
    amc_expiry      DATE,
    ytd_breakdown   BIGINT,
    ytd_downtime_hrs NUMERIC,
    ytd_maint_cost  NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        er.equipment_name,
        er.category,
        er.serial_no,
        w.name                                              AS location,
        er.status,
        (SELECT MAX(mwo.completed_at::DATE) FROM facility.maintenance_work_orders mwo
         WHERE mwo.equipment_id = er.id AND mwo.status = 'completed') AS last_maintained,
        (SELECT MIN(ms.next_due_date) FROM facility.maintenance_schedules ms
         WHERE ms.equipment_id = er.id AND ms.is_active = TRUE) AS next_pm_due,
        er.next_calibration                                 AS calibration_due,
        er.warranty_expiry,
        er.amc_end_date                                     AS amc_expiry,
        -- YTD stats
        COUNT(CASE WHEN mwo2.wo_type = 'breakdown'
              AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM mwo2.reported_at) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
              THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS ytd_breakdown,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(YEAR FROM mwo2.reported_at) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
              THEN COALESCE(mwo2.downtime_hours, 0) ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS ytd_downtime_hrs,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(YEAR FROM mwo2.reported_at) = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())
              THEN COALESCE(mwo2.total_cost, 0) ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS ytd_maint_cost
    FROM facility.equipment_registry er
    LEFT JOIN facility.wards w ON er.location_ward_id = w.id
    LEFT JOIN facility.maintenance_work_orders mwo2 ON mwo2.equipment_id = er.id
    WHERE er.branch_id = p_branch_id AND er.status != 'condemned'
    GROUP BY er.equipment_name, er.category, er.serial_no, w.name,
             er.status, er.next_calibration, er.warranty_expiry, er.amc_end_date, er.id
    ORDER BY er.category, er.equipment_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 15: BLOOD BANK INVENTORY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_blood_bank_report(
    p_branch_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    blood_group         TEXT,
    component           TEXT,
    available_units     BIGINT,
    expiring_7_days     BIGINT,
    expiring_30_days    BIGINT,
    expired_units       BIGINT,
    issued_this_month   BIGINT,
    requests_pending    BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        bbi.blood_group,
        bbi.component,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS available_units,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available'
              AND bbi.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 7 THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS expiring_7_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available'
              AND bbi.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 30 THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS expiring_30_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available'
              AND bbi.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS expired_units,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'issued'
              AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM bi.issued_at) = EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NOW()) THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS issued_this_month,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM operations.blood_requests br2
         WHERE br2.branch_id = p_branch_id
           AND br2.blood_group = bbi.blood_group
           AND br2.component = bbi.component
           AND br2.status = 'pending')::BIGINT AS requests_pending
    FROM operations.blood_bank_inventory bbi
    LEFT JOIN operations.blood_issues bi ON bi.bag_id = bbi.id
    WHERE bbi.branch_id = p_branch_id
    GROUP BY bbi.blood_group, bbi.component
    ORDER BY bbi.blood_group, bbi.component;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 16: COMPLAINT ANALYSIS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_complaint_analysis(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    category_name       TEXT,
    total_complaints    BIGINT,
    resolved            BIGINT,
    pending             BIGINT,
    escalated           BIGINT,
    sla_breached        BIGINT,
    avg_resolution_hrs  NUMERIC,
    satisfaction_rate   NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        cc.name                                             AS category_name,
        COUNT(c.id)::BIGINT                                AS total_complaints,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.status IN ('resolved', 'closed') THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS resolved,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.status = 'open' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS pending,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.status = 'escalated' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS escalated,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN c.sla_breach = TRUE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS sla_breached,
        ROUND(AVG(
            CASE WHEN c.resolved_at IS NOT NULL
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (c.resolved_at - c.created_at)) / 3600
            END
        )::NUMERIC, 1)                                     AS avg_resolution_hrs,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN c.complainant_satisfied = TRUE THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(CASE WHEN c.complainant_satisfied IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                         AS satisfaction_rate
    FROM operations.complaints c
    JOIN operations.complaint_categories cc ON c.category_id = cc.id
    WHERE c.org_id = p_org_id
      AND c.created_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY cc.name
    ORDER BY total_complaints DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 17: TRIAL BALANCE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_trial_balance(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_as_of     DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    account_code    TEXT,
    account_name    TEXT,
    account_type    TEXT,
    debit_balance   NUMERIC,
    credit_balance  NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        coa.account_code,
        coa.account_name,
        coa.account_type,
        CASE WHEN coa.balance >= 0 THEN ROUND(coa.balance::NUMERIC, 2) ELSE 0 END AS debit_balance,
        CASE WHEN coa.balance < 0  THEN ROUND(ABS(coa.balance)::NUMERIC, 2) ELSE 0 END AS credit_balance
    FROM finance.chart_of_accounts coa
    WHERE coa.org_id = p_org_id
      AND coa.is_active = TRUE
      AND coa.balance != 0
    ORDER BY coa.account_code;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 18: MULTI-BRANCH COMPARISON REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_branch_comparison(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_month     INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_year      INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    branch_name         TEXT,
    total_admissions    BIGINT,
    total_discharges    BIGINT,
    avg_bed_occupancy   NUMERIC,
    opd_visits          BIGINT,
    total_revenue       NUMERIC,
    total_collected     NUMERIC,
    pharmacy_revenue    NUMERIC,
    lab_revenue         NUMERIC,
    total_expenses      NUMERIC,
    net_income          NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        br.name                                             AS branch_name,
        -- Clinical
        COUNT(DISTINCT a.id)::BIGINT AS total_admissions,
        COUNT(DISTINCT d.id)::BIGINT AS total_discharges,
        ROUND(AVG(
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM facility.beds b2
             JOIN facility.wards w2 ON b2.ward_id = w2.id
             WHERE w2.branch_id = br.id AND b2.status = 'occupied')::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM facility.beds b3
                    JOIN facility.wards w3 ON b3.ward_id = w3.id
                    WHERE w3.branch_id = br.id AND b3.is_active = TRUE), 0) * 100
        )::NUMERIC, 1) AS avg_bed_occupancy,
        COUNT(DISTINCT pv.id) FILTER (WHERE pv.visit_type = 'opd')::BIGINT AS opd_visits,
        -- Finance
        ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 0) AS total_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount)::NUMERIC, 0) AS total_collected,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'pharmacy' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 0) AS pharmacy_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'lab' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 0) AS lab_revenue,
        0::NUMERIC AS total_expenses,  -- from journal entries
        ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount)::NUMERIC, 0) AS net_income
    FROM platform.branches br
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.branch_id = br.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM a.admission_datetime) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM a.admission_datetime) = p_year
    LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM d.discharge_datetime) = p_month
    LEFT JOIN clinical.patient_visits pv ON pv.branch_id = br.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM pv.visit_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM pv.visit_date) = p_year
    LEFT JOIN finance.bills b ON b.branch_id = br.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM b.bill_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM b.bill_date) = p_year
        AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND br.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY br.id, br.name
    ORDER BY total_revenue DESC NULLS LAST;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## 📊 COMPLETE FINAL SUMMARY

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- SYSTEM HEALTH CHECK FUNCTION
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_system_health_check(p_org_id UUID)
RETURNS TABLE (
    check_name      TEXT,
    status          TEXT,
    count_value     BIGINT,
    action_needed   TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY

    -- Expired medicines
    SELECT 'Expired Medicines'::TEXT, '🔴'::TEXT,
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Discard expired stock immediately'::TEXT
    FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
    JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON ms.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE AND ms.quantity > 0

    UNION ALL

    -- Pending critical lab results
    SELECT 'Unnotified Critical Results', '🔴',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Notify doctors immediately'
    FROM clinical.lab_results lr
    JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON lr.order_item_id IN (
        SELECT id FROM clinical.lab_order_items WHERE order_id = lo.id
    )
    JOIN platform.branches br ON lo.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND lr.is_critical = TRUE
      AND lr.critical_notified_at IS NULL
      AND lr.entered_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'

    UNION ALL

    -- Overdue maintenance
    SELECT 'Overdue PM Schedules', '🟡',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Schedule preventive maintenance'
    FROM facility.maintenance_schedules ms
    JOIN facility.equipment_registry er ON ms.equipment_id = er.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON er.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND ms.next_due_date < CURRENT_DATE
      AND ms.is_active = TRUE

    UNION ALL

    -- SLA breached complaints
    SELECT 'SLA Breached Complaints', '🟡',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Escalate unresolved complaints'
    FROM operations.complaints c
    WHERE c.org_id = p_org_id
      AND c.sla_breach = TRUE
      AND c.status NOT IN ('resolved', 'closed')

    UNION ALL

    -- Missing consents for today surgeries
    SELECT 'Surgeries Without Consent', '🔴',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Obtain patient consent before proceeding'
    FROM clinical.surgeries s
    WHERE s.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
      AND s.scheduled_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
      AND s.status = 'scheduled'
      AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM clinical.patient_consents pc
          WHERE pc.surgery_id = s.id AND pc.is_withdrawn = FALSE
      )

    UNION ALL

    -- Beds in cleaning > 2 hours
    SELECT 'Beds Overdue Cleaning', '🟡',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        'Follow up with housekeeping'
    FROM facility.bed_cleaning_tasks bct
    JOIN facility.beds b ON bct.bed_id = b.id
    JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
    WHERE w.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
      AND bct.status = 'pending'
      AND bct.assigned_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '2 hours'

    ORDER BY 1;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

# Hospital ERP - Complete PostgreSQL Schema Design

## Architecture Overview

text

```
PLATFORM (Multi-Tenant)
    └── ORGANIZATIONS
            └── BRANCHES
                    ├── CLINICAL MODULES
                    ├── FINANCE MODULES
                    ├── OPERATIONS MODULES
                    └── SECURITY MODULES
```

---

## 📦 SCHEMA ORGANIZATION

SQL

```
-- Create Schemas for Module Grouping
CREATE SCHEMA platform;      -- Multi-tenancy, Auth, Roles
CREATE SCHEMA clinical;      -- Patient, EMR, OT, Nursing, Diagnostic
CREATE SCHEMA finance;       -- Payment, Insurance, Accounts
CREATE SCHEMA pharmacy;      -- Medicine, Dispensing, Stock
CREATE SCHEMA operations;    -- Kitchen, Housekeeping, Ambulance, Waste
CREATE SCHEMA hr;            -- Employee, Payroll, Roster
CREATE SCHEMA facility;      -- Building, Maintenance, Asset
CREATE SCHEMA analytics;     -- Reports, Dashboard, KPI
CREATE SCHEMA audit;         -- Audit Log, Security
```

---

## 🔵 PLATFORM SCHEMA

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- PLATFORM: ORGANIZATIONS & BRANCHES (Multi-Tenancy)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE platform.organizations (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name                VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    name_bn             VARCHAR(200),                    -- Bangla name
    slug                VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,    -- subdomain
    logo_url            TEXT,
    license_no          VARCHAR(100),
    license_expiry      DATE,
    contact_email       VARCHAR(150),
    contact_phone       VARCHAR(20),
    address             TEXT,
    country             VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladesh',
    timezone            VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'Asia/Dhaka',
    currency            VARCHAR(10)  DEFAULT 'BDT',
    subscription_plan   VARCHAR(50),                    -- basic/pro/enterprise
    subscription_expiry DATE,
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    settings            JSONB DEFAULT '{}',             -- org-level config
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE platform.branches (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    name_bn         VARCHAR(200),
    code            VARCHAR(20),
    branch_type     VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'hospital',   -- hospital/clinic/diagnostic
    address         TEXT,
    city            VARCHAR(100),
    phone           VARCHAR(20),
    email           VARCHAR(150),
    bed_capacity    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    settings        JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(org_id, code)
);

-- ============================================================
-- PLATFORM: ROLES & PERMISSIONS (RBAC)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE platform.roles (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    slug            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    is_system       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,  -- system roles can't be deleted
    parent_role_id  UUID REFERENCES platform.roles(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(org_id, slug)
);

-- Seed system roles
INSERT INTO platform.roles (name, slug, is_system, org_id) VALUES
('Super Admin',           'super_admin',          TRUE, NULL),
('Hospital Admin',        'hospital_admin',        TRUE, NULL),
('Branch Admin',          'branch_admin',          TRUE, NULL),
('Doctor',                'doctor',                TRUE, NULL),
('Senior Doctor',         'senior_doctor',         TRUE, NULL),
('Nurse',                 'nurse',                 TRUE, NULL),
('Head Nurse',            'head_nurse',            TRUE, NULL),
('Pharmacist',            'pharmacist',            TRUE, NULL),
('Lab Technician',        'lab_technician',        TRUE, NULL),
('Radiologist',           'radiologist',           TRUE, NULL),
('Receptionist',          'receptionist',          TRUE, NULL),
('Cashier',               'cashier',               TRUE, NULL),
('Insurance Executive',   'insurance_executive',   TRUE, NULL),
('HR Manager',            'hr_manager',            TRUE, NULL),
('Accountant',            'accountant',            TRUE, NULL),
('Store Manager',         'store_manager',         TRUE, NULL),
('Housekeeping Supervisor','housekeeping_supervisor',TRUE, NULL),
('Maintenance Technician','maintenance_technician', TRUE, NULL),
('Dietitian',             'dietitian',             TRUE, NULL),
('Ambulance Driver',      'ambulance_driver',      TRUE, NULL),
('Blood Bank Technician', 'blood_bank_technician', TRUE, NULL),
('Report Viewer',         'report_viewer',         TRUE, NULL);

CREATE TABLE platform.permissions (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    module      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,   -- e.g. 'clinical.patients'
    action      VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,   -- create/read/update/delete/export/approve
    description TEXT,
    UNIQUE(module, action)
);

CREATE TABLE platform.role_permissions (
    role_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.roles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    permission_id UUID REFERENCES platform.permissions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    granted_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    PRIMARY KEY (role_id, permission_id)
);

-- ============================================================
-- PLATFORM: USERS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE platform.users (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    role_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.roles(id),
    employee_id         UUID,                           -- FK to hr.employees (set later)
    username            VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    email               VARCHAR(150) UNIQUE,
    phone               VARCHAR(20),
    password_hash       TEXT NOT NULL,
    full_name           VARCHAR(200),
    avatar_url          TEXT,
    language            VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'en',
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_2fa_enabled      BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    two_fa_secret       TEXT,
    last_login_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    password_changed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    force_password_reset BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    failed_login_count  INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    locked_until        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    settings            JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE platform.user_sessions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    user_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    token_hash      TEXT NOT NULL,
    device_info     JSONB,
    ip_address      INET,
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    revoked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE platform.user_branch_access (
    user_id     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    branch_id   UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    granted_at  TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    granted_by  UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    PRIMARY KEY (user_id, branch_id)
);
```
