## 🔵 CLINICAL SCHEMA — PATIENT MANAGEMENT

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: PATIENTS (Core)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.patients (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    mrn                 VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,     -- Medical Record Number
    barcode             VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE,
    qr_code             TEXT,
    
    -- Demographics
    first_name          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name           VARCHAR(100),
    full_name           VARCHAR(200),
    full_name_bn        VARCHAR(200),                   -- Bangla name
    date_of_birth       DATE,
    age_years           INTEGER,                        -- if DOB unknown
    gender              VARCHAR(20),                    -- male/female/other
    blood_group         VARCHAR(10),
    
    -- Contact
    phone               VARCHAR(20),
    phone_alt           VARCHAR(20),
    email               VARCHAR(150),
    address             TEXT,
    district            VARCHAR(100),
    division            VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Identity
    nid_no              VARCHAR(30),                    -- Encrypted
    passport_no         VARCHAR(30),
    visa_no             VARCHAR(50),
    visa_expiry         DATE,
    
    -- Category
    patient_type        VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'regular',  -- regular/vip/corporate/foreigner
    nationality         VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladeshi',
    religion            VARCHAR(50),
    occupation          VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Family
    father_name         VARCHAR(200),
    mother_name         VARCHAR(200),
    spouse_name         VARCHAR(200),
    guardian_name       VARCHAR(200),
    guardian_relation   VARCHAR(50),
    guardian_phone      VARCHAR(20),
    family_group_id     UUID,                           -- for family linking
    
    -- Special flags
    is_mlc              BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,          -- Medico-Legal Case
    is_vip              BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_deceased         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    death_datetime      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Media
    photo_url           TEXT,
    
    -- Insurance
    insurance_company   VARCHAR(200),
    policy_number       VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- System
    registered_by       UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    duplicate_of        UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id), -- for merge
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-generate MRN
CREATE SEQUENCE clinical.mrn_seq START 100001;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.generate_mrn()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.mrn IS NULL THEN
        NEW.mrn := 'MRN' || LPAD(nextval('clinical.mrn_seq')::TEXT, 7, '0');
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patient_mrn
BEFORE INSERT ON clinical.patients
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.generate_mrn();

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: PATIENT MEDICAL HISTORY
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_medical_history (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    history_type    VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- pmh/psh/family/social/chronic
    condition       VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    onset_date      DATE,
    notes           TEXT,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    recorded_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_allergies (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    allergen_type   VARCHAR(50),  -- drug/food/environment
    allergen_name   VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    reaction        VARCHAR(300),
    severity        VARCHAR(20),  -- mild/moderate/severe/life-threatening
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active',
    recorded_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_immunizations (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    vaccine_name    VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    dose_no         INTEGER,
    given_date      DATE,
    next_due_date   DATE,
    given_by        VARCHAR(200),
    batch_no        VARCHAR(100),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: VISIT LOG
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_visits (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    visit_type      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- opd/ipd/emergency/telemedicine
    visit_date      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    visit_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    chief_complaint TEXT,
    status          VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active', -- active/completed/cancelled
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: ADMISSIONS (IPD)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.admissions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    visit_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    admission_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    admission_datetime  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    admission_type      VARCHAR(30), -- elective/emergency/transfer
    
    -- Bed Assignment
    ward_id             UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    bed_id              UUID REFERENCES facility.beds(id),
    
    -- Clinical
    admitting_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    primary_diagnosis   VARCHAR(500),
    icd10_code          VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Status
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'admitted', -- admitted/discharged/transferred/absconded/deceased
    discharge_datetime  TIMESTAMPTZ,
    length_of_stay      INTEGER,                        -- auto-computed trigger
    
    -- Insurance
    insurance_id        UUID REFERENCES finance.patient_insurance_policies(id),
    pre_auth_no         VARCHAR(100),
    
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto compute LOS on discharge
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.compute_los()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.discharge_datetime IS NOT NULL AND OLD.discharge_datetime IS NULL THEN
        NEW.length_of_stay := EXTRACT(DAY FROM (NEW.discharge_datetime - NEW.admission_datetime))::INTEGER;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_admission_los
BEFORE UPDATE ON clinical.admissions
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.compute_los();

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: DISCHARGE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.discharges (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    admission_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    discharge_datetime  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    discharge_type      VARCHAR(50), -- regular/lama/absconded/deceased/transfer
    discharge_condition VARCHAR(50), -- improved/stable/deteriorated/deceased
    final_diagnosis     TEXT,
    icd10_code          VARCHAR(20),
    discharge_summary   TEXT,
    follow_up_date      DATE,
    follow_up_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    discharge_by        UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: VITAL SIGNS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.vital_signs (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    recorded_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    recorded_at     TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    -- Vitals
    bp_systolic     INTEGER,
    bp_diastolic    INTEGER,
    pulse_rate      INTEGER,
    temperature     DECIMAL(5,2),
    temp_unit       VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'C',
    spo2            DECIMAL(5,2),
    respiratory_rate INTEGER,
    weight_kg       DECIMAL(8,2),
    height_cm       DECIMAL(6,2),
    bmi             DECIMAL(5,2),
    gcs_score       INTEGER,
    pain_scale      INTEGER,            -- 0-10
    blood_glucose   DECIMAL(6,2),
    notes           TEXT
);

-- Auto-calculate BMI
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.calc_bmi()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.weight_kg IS NOT NULL AND NEW.height_cm IS NOT NULL AND NEW.height_cm > 0 THEN
        NEW.bmi := ROUND((NEW.weight_kg / POWER(NEW.height_cm/100, 2))::NUMERIC, 2);
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_vital_bmi
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON clinical.vital_signs
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.calc_bmi();

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: EMR / CLINICAL NOTES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.clinical_notes (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    note_type       VARCHAR(50), -- progress/nursing/soap/discharge_summary
    note_date       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    subjective      TEXT,
    objective       TEXT,
    assessment      TEXT,
    plan            TEXT,
    full_note       TEXT,
    authored_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    signed_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    is_final        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: DIAGNOSES (ICD-10)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.icd10_codes (
    code        VARCHAR(20) PRIMARY KEY,
    description VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    category    VARCHAR(200),
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_diagnoses (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    icd10_code      VARCHAR(20) REFERENCES clinical.icd10_codes(code),
    description     VARCHAR(500),
    diagnosis_type  VARCHAR(30),    -- primary/secondary/working/confirmed
    diagnosed_by    UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    diagnosed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

## 🔵 CLINICAL — OT MANAGEMENT

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: OT ROOMS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.ot_rooms (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    ot_type         VARCHAR(50),  -- major/minor/emergency/cardiac/neuro
    capacity        INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    equipment_list  JSONB DEFAULT '[]'
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: SURGERIES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.surgeries (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id               UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    ot_room_id              UUID REFERENCES clinical.ot_rooms(id),
    
    -- Scheduling
    scheduled_datetime      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    surgery_type            VARCHAR(50), -- elective/emergency/daycare
    surgery_name            VARCHAR(300),
    icd10_code              VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Team
    primary_surgeon_id      UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    assistant_surgeon_id    UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    anesthetist_id          UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    scrub_nurse_id          UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    circulating_nurse_id    UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    
    -- Timings
    actual_start_time       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    actual_end_time         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_minutes        INTEGER,
    
    -- Anesthesia
    anesthesia_type         VARCHAR(50), -- general/spinal/local/epidural
    
    -- Intra-op
    blood_loss_ml           INTEGER,
    instrument_count_ok     BOOLEAN,
    swab_count_ok           BOOLEAN,
    specimen_sent           BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- WHO Checklist
    who_sign_in_done        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    who_time_out_done       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    who_sign_out_done       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Status
    status                  VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    cancellation_reason     TEXT,
    
    notes                   TEXT,
    created_by              UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Compute surgery duration trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.compute_surgery_duration()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.actual_start_time IS NOT NULL AND NEW.actual_end_time IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.duration_minutes := EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NEW.actual_end_time - NEW.actual_start_time))/60;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_surgery_duration
BEFORE UPDATE ON clinical.surgeries
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.compute_surgery_duration();

CREATE TABLE clinical.surgery_implants (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    surgery_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.surgeries(id),
    implant_name VARCHAR(200),
    brand        VARCHAR(100),
    lot_no       VARCHAR(100),
    size         VARCHAR(50),
    quantity     INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    cost         DECIMAL(12,2)
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: ANESTHESIA RECORDS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.anesthesia_records (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    surgery_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.surgeries(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    anesthetist_id      UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    pre_assessment_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    asa_grade           VARCHAR(5),   -- ASA I-VI
    technique           VARCHAR(50),
    drugs_used          JSONB DEFAULT '[]', -- [{drug, dose, route, time}]
    monitoring_data     JSONB DEFAULT '[]', -- [{time, bp, pr, spo2}]
    total_fluid_ml      INTEGER,
    recovery_notes      TEXT,
    aldrete_score       INTEGER,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

## 🔵 CLINICAL — NURSE STATION

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: NURSING ASSESSMENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.nursing_assessments (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    assessed_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    assessed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    -- Assessments
    fall_risk_score     INTEGER,
    fall_risk_level     VARCHAR(20),  -- low/medium/high
    braden_score        INTEGER,      -- pressure ulcer
    nutrition_score     INTEGER,
    pain_location       VARCHAR(200),
    pain_character      VARCHAR(100),
    
    notes               TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.nursing_care_plans (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    problem         VARCHAR(300),
    goal            TEXT,
    intervention    TEXT,
    evaluation      TEXT,
    created_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    review_date     DATE,
    status          VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: MEDICATION ADMINISTRATION RECORD (MAR)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.medication_orders (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    ordered_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),  -- doctor
    ordered_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    drug_name           VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    generic_name        VARCHAR(300),
    dose                VARCHAR(100),
    route               VARCHAR(50),   -- oral/iv/im/sc/topical
    frequency           VARCHAR(100),  -- BD/TDS/QID/PRN/STAT
    start_datetime      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    end_datetime        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_days       INTEGER,
    instructions        TEXT,
    is_stat             BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_prn              BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_narcotic         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active', -- active/completed/discontinued/hold
    discontinued_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discontinued_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    discontinue_reason  TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.medication_administrations (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_id            UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.medication_orders(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    administered_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),  -- nurse
    administered_at     TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    -- Five Rights Verification
    patient_verified    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    drug_verified       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    dose_verified       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    route_verified      BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    time_verified       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    actual_dose         VARCHAR(100),
    actual_route        VARCHAR(50),
    status              VARCHAR(30),  -- given/held/refused/missed
    reason_if_not_given TEXT,
    witness_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),  -- for narcotics
    notes               TEXT
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: FLUID BALANCE (I&O Chart)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.fluid_records (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    recorded_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    recorded_at     TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    record_type     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- intake/output
    category        VARCHAR(50),  -- iv_fluid/oral/urine/drain/ng_tube
    description     VARCHAR(200),
    amount_ml       INTEGER NOT NULL
);

-- Daily Fluid Balance View
CREATE VIEW clinical.v_daily_fluid_balance AS
SELECT
    patient_id,
    admission_id,
    DATE(recorded_at) AS record_date,
    SUM(CASE WHEN record_type = 'intake' THEN amount_ml ELSE 0 END) AS total_intake_ml,
    SUM(CASE WHEN record_type = 'output' THEN amount_ml ELSE 0 END) AS total_output_ml,
    SUM(CASE WHEN record_type = 'intake' THEN amount_ml ELSE -amount_ml END) AS balance_ml
FROM clinical.fluid_records
GROUP BY patient_id, admission_id, DATE(recorded_at);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: SHIFT & HANDOVER
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.nurse_shifts (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    ward_id         UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    nurse_id        UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    shift_type      VARCHAR(20), -- morning/evening/night
    shift_date      DATE NOT NULL,
    start_time      TIME,
    end_time        TIME,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'scheduled'
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.shift_handovers (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ward_id             UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    shift_date          DATE,
    from_shift          VARCHAR(20),
    to_shift            VARCHAR(20),
    handover_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    received_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    handover_notes      TEXT,
    pending_tasks       JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    critical_patients   JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    handover_datetime   TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

## 🔵 CLINICAL — DIAGNOSTIC

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: TEST CATALOG
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.test_categories (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    type        VARCHAR(30),  -- lab/radiology/microbiology/histopathology/poc
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.test_master (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES clinical.test_categories(id),
    test_code           VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    test_name           VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    short_name          VARCHAR(50),
    specimen_type       VARCHAR(100),  -- blood/urine/stool/tissue/swab
    container_type      VARCHAR(100),  -- EDTA/plain/heparin
    volume_required     VARCHAR(50),
    method              VARCHAR(100),
    tat_hours           INTEGER,       -- Turnaround time
    price               DECIMAL(12,2),
    cost                DECIMAL(12,2),
    is_critical         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    sort_order          INTEGER DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.test_reference_ranges (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    test_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.test_master(id),
    gender          VARCHAR(10),       -- male/female/all
    age_min_years   DECIMAL(5,2),
    age_max_years   DECIMAL(5,2),
    normal_low      DECIMAL(15,4),
    normal_high     DECIMAL(15,4),
    critical_low    DECIMAL(15,4),
    critical_high   DECIMAL(15,4),
    unit            VARCHAR(50),
    interpretation  TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.test_panels (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    panel_name  VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    panel_price DECIMAL(12,2),
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.test_panel_items (
    panel_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.test_panels(id),
    test_id     UUID REFERENCES clinical.test_master(id),
    PRIMARY KEY (panel_id, test_id)
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: LAB ORDERS & SAMPLES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.lab_orders (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    order_no            VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    ordered_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    ordered_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'routine', -- routine/urgent/stat
    clinical_info       TEXT,
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'pending'  -- pending/collected/processing/resulted/verified
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.lab_order_items (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.lab_orders(id),
    test_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.test_master(id),
    panel_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.test_panels(id),
    price       DECIMAL(12,2),
    status      VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'pending'
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.lab_samples (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_id            UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.lab_orders(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    barcode             VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE,
    specimen_type       VARCHAR(100),
    container_type      VARCHAR(100),
    collected_by        UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    collected_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    received_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    quality_status      VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'acceptable', -- acceptable/rejected
    rejection_reason    VARCHAR(200),
    storage_location    VARCHAR(100),
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'pending'
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.lab_results (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_item_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.lab_order_items(id),
    sample_id           UUID REFERENCES clinical.lab_samples(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    test_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.test_master(id),
    
    result_value        VARCHAR(300),
    result_numeric      DECIMAL(15,4),
    result_unit         VARCHAR(50),
    result_text         TEXT,         -- for micro/histo
    
    -- Flags
    abnormal_flag       VARCHAR(10),  -- H/L/HH/LL/A (abnormal)
    is_critical         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    reference_range     VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Workflow
    entered_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    entered_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    verified_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    verified_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Critical notification
    critical_notified_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    critical_notified_to    UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'draft'   -- draft/final/amended
);

-- Critical value auto-alert trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.check_critical_result()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.is_critical = TRUE AND OLD.is_critical = FALSE THEN
        INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
            notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
            message, priority, created_at
        ) VALUES (
            'critical_lab_result', NEW.id, 'clinical.lab_results',
            'CRITICAL LAB RESULT - Patient requires immediate attention',
            'urgent', NOW()
        );
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_critical_lab_alert
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON clinical.lab_results
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.check_critical_result();
```

## 🔵 FACILITY SCHEMA — BED & WARD

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- FACILITY: BUILDING, FLOOR, WARD, BED
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE facility.buildings (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id   UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    code        VARCHAR(20),
    floors      INTEGER,
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE facility.floors (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.buildings(id),
    floor_no    INTEGER NOT NULL,
    floor_name  VARCHAR(100),
    floor_plan  TEXT                -- URL to floor plan image
);

CREATE TABLE facility.wards (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    floor_id        UUID REFERENCES facility.floors(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    code            VARCHAR(20),
    ward_type       VARCHAR(50),    -- general/private/icu/nicu/maternity/pediatric/surgical
    gender_type     VARCHAR(20),    -- male/female/mixed/pediatric
    bed_capacity    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE facility.rooms (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ward_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    room_no     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    room_type   VARCHAR(30),        -- single/double/suite/bay
    floor_id    UUID REFERENCES facility.floors(id),
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE facility.beds (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ward_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    room_id         UUID REFERENCES facility.rooms(id),
    bed_no          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    bed_type        VARCHAR(50),    -- standard/electric/icu/nicu/isolation
    features        JSONB DEFAULT '[]',   -- ['oxygen','monitor','rails','iv_pole']
    daily_rate      DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Status (Real-time)
    status          VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'available',
                    -- available/occupied/reserved/cleaning/maintenance
    
    current_patient_id UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    current_admission_id UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Real-Time Bed Status View
CREATE VIEW facility.v_bed_status AS
SELECT
    b.id AS bed_id,
    b.bed_no,
    b.bed_type,
    b.status,
    b.daily_rate,
    w.name AS ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    r.room_no,
    br.name AS branch_name,
    p.full_name AS patient_name,
    p.mrn,
    a.admission_datetime,
    EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - a.admission_datetime) AS days_admitted
FROM facility.beds b
JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
LEFT JOIN facility.rooms r ON b.room_id = r.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.patients p ON b.current_patient_id = p.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON b.current_admission_id = a.id;

-- ============================================================
-- FACILITY: BED TRANSFERS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE facility.bed_transfers (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    from_ward_id        UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    from_bed_id         UUID REFERENCES facility.beds(id),
    to_ward_id          UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    to_bed_id           UUID REFERENCES facility.beds(id),
    transfer_reason     TEXT,
    transfer_type       VARCHAR(50),  -- escalation/step_down/request/discharge_prep
    transferred_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    transferred_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    notes               TEXT
);

-- Update bed status on transfer trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.handle_bed_transfer()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Free old bed
    UPDATE facility.beds
    SET status = 'cleaning',
        current_patient_id = NULL,
        current_admission_id = NULL
    WHERE id = NEW.from_bed_id;
    
    -- Occupy new bed
    UPDATE facility.beds
    SET status = 'occupied',
        current_patient_id = NEW.patient_id,
        current_admission_id = NEW.admission_id
    WHERE id = NEW.to_bed_id;
    
    -- Update admission record
    UPDATE clinical.admissions
    SET ward_id = NEW.to_ward_id,
        bed_id = NEW.to_bed_id
    WHERE id = NEW.admission_id;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_bed_transfer
AFTER INSERT ON facility.bed_transfers
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.handle_bed_transfer();

-- ============================================================
-- FACILITY: BED CLEANING TASKS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE facility.bed_cleaning_tasks (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    bed_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.beds(id),
    trigger_type        VARCHAR(30),  -- discharge/transfer/scheduled
    assigned_to         UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    assigned_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    started_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    verified_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    checklist           JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending'
);

-- Auto-create cleaning task on discharge trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION facility.auto_cleaning_on_discharge()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_bed_id UUID;
BEGIN
    SELECT bed_id INTO v_bed_id FROM clinical.admissions WHERE id = NEW.admission_id;
    
    IF v_bed_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        INSERT INTO facility.bed_cleaning_tasks(bed_id, trigger_type)
        VALUES (v_bed_id, 'discharge');
        
        UPDATE facility.beds SET status = 'cleaning' WHERE id = v_bed_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_auto_cleaning
AFTER INSERT ON clinical.discharges
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION facility.auto_cleaning_on_discharge();
```

## 🔵 PHARMACY SCHEMA

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- PHARMACY: MEDICINE CATALOG
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.medicine_categories (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.medicines (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES pharmacy.medicine_categories(id),
    generic_name        VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    brand_name          VARCHAR(300),
    strength            VARCHAR(100),
    form                VARCHAR(50),    -- tablet/capsule/injection/syrup/cream
    route               VARCHAR(50),
    manufacturer        VARCHAR(200),
    barcode             VARCHAR(50),
    dgda_reg_no         VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Flags
    is_controlled       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_narcotic         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_high_alert       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_antibiotic       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    pregnancy_category  VARCHAR(5),   -- A/B/C/D/X
    
    -- Pricing
    purchase_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    sale_price          DECIMAL(12,2),
    
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- PHARMACY: STOCK MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.medicine_stock (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    medicine_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.medicines(id),
    batch_no            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    expiry_date         DATE NOT NULL,
    quantity            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    purchase_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    mrp                 DECIMAL(12,2),
    location            VARCHAR(100),   -- shelf/rack
    received_date       DATE,
    supplier_id         UUID REFERENCES finance.suppliers(id),
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active',   -- active/expired/recalled
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(branch_id, medicine_id, batch_no)
);

-- Low stock alert trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION pharmacy.check_low_stock()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_min_stock INTEGER := 10;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.quantity <= v_min_stock AND OLD.quantity > v_min_stock THEN
        INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
            notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
            message, priority, created_at
        ) VALUES (
            'low_stock', NEW.id, 'pharmacy.medicine_stock',
            'Low stock alert for medicine batch',
            'high', NOW()
        );
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_low_stock
AFTER UPDATE ON pharmacy.medicine_stock
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION pharmacy.check_low_stock();

-- ============================================================
-- PHARMACY: PRESCRIPTIONS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.prescriptions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    visit_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    prescribed_by       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    prescription_type   VARCHAR(30),  -- opd/ipd/discharge/emergency/telemedicine
    rx_no               VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    prescribed_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    valid_until         DATE,
    notes               TEXT,
    digital_signature   TEXT,
    qr_code             TEXT,
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'active'
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.prescription_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.prescriptions(id),
    medicine_id     UUID REFERENCES pharmacy.medicines(id),
    drug_name       VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,    -- allow free text
    dose            VARCHAR(100),
    route           VARCHAR(50),
    frequency       VARCHAR(100),
    duration        VARCHAR(100),
    quantity        INTEGER,
    instructions    TEXT,
    is_substitutable BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- ============================================================
-- PHARMACY: DISPENSING
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.dispensings (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    prescription_id     UUID REFERENCES pharmacy.prescriptions(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    dispensed_by        UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    dispensed_at        TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    dispensing_type     VARCHAR(30),  -- opd/ipd_floor/discharge/counter_sale
    total_amount        DECIMAL(12,2),
    discount_amount     DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount          DECIMAL(12,2),
    notes               TEXT,
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'complete'
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.dispensing_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    dispensing_id   UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.dispensings(id),
    rx_item_id      UUID REFERENCES pharmacy.prescription_items(id),
    medicine_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.medicines(id),
    stock_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.medicine_stock(id),
    batch_no        VARCHAR(100),
    quantity        INTEGER NOT NULL,
    unit_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    total_price     DECIMAL(12,2),
    is_substitution BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
);

-- Reduce stock on dispensing trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION pharmacy.reduce_stock_on_dispensing()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE pharmacy.medicine_stock
    SET quantity = quantity - NEW.quantity
    WHERE id = NEW.stock_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reduce_stock
AFTER INSERT ON pharmacy.dispensing_items
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION pharmacy.reduce_stock_on_dispensing();
```

## 🔵 FINANCE SCHEMA

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: SUPPLIERS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.suppliers (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    code            VARCHAR(30),
    category        VARCHAR(50),    -- medicine/equipment/consumable/food
    contact_person  VARCHAR(200),
    phone           VARCHAR(20),
    email           VARCHAR(150),
    address         TEXT,
    trade_license   VARCHAR(100),
    bin_no          VARCHAR(50),
    tin_no          VARCHAR(50),
    credit_days     INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
    credit_limit    DECIMAL(14,2),
    rating          INTEGER,        -- 1-5
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: BILLING
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.bills (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    bill_no             VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    bill_date           TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    bill_type           VARCHAR(30),  -- opd/ipd/emergency/pharmacy
    
    -- Amounts
    gross_amount        DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount     DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount          DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount          DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    paid_amount         DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    insurance_amount    DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    balance_amount      DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Status
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'draft',   -- draft/final/paid/partial/cancelled
    
    -- Insurance
    insurance_id        UUID REFERENCES finance.patient_insurance_policies(id),
    
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    finalized_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.bill_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    bill_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.bills(id),
    item_type       VARCHAR(50),    -- bed/medicine/lab/radiology/procedure/ot/doctor_fee
    item_name       VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    item_ref_id     UUID,           -- FK to respective module
    quantity        DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 1,
    unit_price      DECIMAL(12,2),
    discount_pct    DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_pct         DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount      DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount      DECIMAL(12,2),
    service_date    DATE
);

-- Auto-update bill balance trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.update_bill_balance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE finance.bills
    SET
        gross_amount = (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(quantity * unit_price), 0) FROM finance.bill_items WHERE bill_id = NEW.bill_id),
        net_amount   = (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(net_amount), 0) FROM finance.bill_items WHERE bill_id = NEW.bill_id),
        balance_amount = net_amount - paid_amount - insurance_amount,
        updated_at   = NOW()
    WHERE id = NEW.bill_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_bill_balance
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON finance.bill_items
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.update_bill_balance();

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: PAYMENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.payments (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    bill_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.bills(id),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    payment_no      VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    payment_date    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    amount          DECIMAL(14,2) NOT NULL,
    payment_method  VARCHAR(30),    -- cash/card/bank_transfer/bkash/nagad/insurance
    reference_no    VARCHAR(100),
    received_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    notes           TEXT,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'success'   -- success/failed/refunded
);

-- Update bill paid amount after payment
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.update_bill_on_payment()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE finance.bills
    SET paid_amount = (
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) FROM finance.payments
            WHERE bill_id = NEW.bill_id AND status = 'success'
        ),
        balance_amount = net_amount - paid_amount - insurance_amount,
        status = CASE
            WHEN (net_amount - paid_amount - insurance_amount) <= 0 THEN 'paid'
            WHEN paid_amount > 0 THEN 'partial'
            ELSE status
        END,
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = NEW.bill_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_payment_update_bill
AFTER INSERT ON finance.payments
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.update_bill_on_payment();

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: INSURANCE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.insurance_companies (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    short_name      VARCHAR(50),
    company_type    VARCHAR(30),    -- insurance/tpa/corporate
    contact_person  VARCHAR(200),
    phone           VARCHAR(20),
    email           VARCHAR(150),
    address         TEXT,
    portal_url      TEXT,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE finance.patient_insurance_policies (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    company_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.insurance_companies(id),
    policy_no           VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    member_id           VARCHAR(100),
    policy_type         VARCHAR(50),
    sum_insured         DECIMAL(14,2),
    balance_amount      DECIMAL(14,2),
    valid_from          DATE,
    valid_to            DATE,
    is_verified         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    document_url        TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.insurance_claims (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    bill_id             UUID REFERENCES finance.bills(id),
    policy_id           UUID REFERENCES finance.patient_insurance_policies(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    claim_no            VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE,
    pre_auth_no         VARCHAR(100),
    claim_date          DATE,
    claimed_amount      DECIMAL(14,2),
    approved_amount     DECIMAL(14,2),
    settled_amount      DECIMAL(14,2),
    rejection_reason    TEXT,
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'draft',
                        -- draft/submitted/query/approved/partial/rejected/settled
    submitted_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    settled_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto meal order to kitchen on diet prescription
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.auto_kitchen_order_on_diet()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO operations.kitchen_meal_requests(
        branch_id, patient_id, admission_id, diet_type_id,
        start_date, status, created_at
    )
    SELECT
        a.branch_id, NEW.patient_id, NEW.admission_id, NEW.diet_type_id,
        NEW.start_date, 'pending', NOW()
    FROM clinical.admissions a
    WHERE a.id = NEW.admission_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_diet_kitchen_order
AFTER INSERT ON operations.patient_diet_prescriptions
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.auto_kitchen_order_on_diet();

CREATE TABLE operations.kitchen_meal_requests (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id      UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    diet_type_id    UUID REFERENCES operations.diet_types(id),
    start_date      DATE,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

## 🔵 AUDIT SCHEMA

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- AUDIT: COMPREHENSIVE AUDIT LOG
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE audit.audit_logs (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    org_id          UUID,
    user_id         UUID,
    user_name       VARCHAR(200),
    action          VARCHAR(20),        -- INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/SELECT
    schema_name     VARCHAR(100),
    table_name      VARCHAR(100),
    record_id       TEXT,
    old_values      JSONB,
    new_values      JSONB,
    changed_fields  TEXT[],
    ip_address      INET,
    user_agent      TEXT,
    session_id      UUID,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);

-- Monthly partitions for performance
CREATE TABLE audit.audit_logs_2025_01 
    PARTITION OF audit.audit_logs 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2025-01-01') TO ('2025-02-01');

CREATE TABLE audit.audit_logs_2025_02 
    PARTITION OF audit.audit_logs 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2025-02-01') TO ('2025-03-01');

-- Universal audit trigger function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.log_changes()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_old JSONB;
    v_new JSONB;
    v_user_id UUID;
    v_changed TEXT[] := '{}';
    v_key TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Get current user from session variable
    BEGIN
        v_user_id := current_setting('app.current_user_id')::UUID;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        v_user_id := NULL;
    END;
    
    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        v_old := to_jsonb(OLD);
        v_new := NULL;
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        v_old := NULL;
        v_new := to_jsonb(NEW);
    ELSE
        v_old := to_jsonb(OLD);
        v_new := to_jsonb(NEW);
        -- Detect changed fields
        FOR v_key IN SELECT key FROM jsonb_each(v_new)
        LOOP
            IF (v_old->v_key) IS DISTINCT FROM (v_new->v_key) THEN
                v_changed := array_append(v_changed, v_key);
            END IF;
        END LOOP;
    END IF;
    
    INSERT INTO audit.audit_logs(
        user_id, action, schema_name, table_name, record_id,
        old_values, new_values, changed_fields, created_at
    ) VALUES (
        v_user_id, TG_OP, TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME,
        COALESCE(NEW.id::TEXT, OLD.id::TEXT),
        v_old, v_new, v_changed, NOW()
    );
    
    RETURN COALESCE(NEW, OLD);
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Apply audit to key tables
CREATE TRIGGER audit_patients
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clinical.patients
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_changes();

CREATE TRIGGER audit_admissions
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clinical.admissions
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_changes();

CREATE TRIGGER audit_bills
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON finance.bills
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_changes();

CREATE TRIGGER audit_payments
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON finance.payments
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_changes();

CREATE TRIGGER audit_dispensings
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON pharmacy.dispensings
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_changes();

-- ============================================================
-- AUDIT: NOTIFICATIONS QUEUE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE audit.notifications_queue (
    id                  BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    notification_type   VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    reference_id        UUID,
    reference_table     VARCHAR(100),
    recipient_user_id   UUID,
    recipient_role      VARCHAR(100),
    channel             VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'system', -- system/sms/email/whatsapp/push
    message             TEXT NOT NULL,
    priority            VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal', -- low/normal/high/urgent
    is_sent             BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    sent_at             TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message       TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- AUDIT: LOGIN HISTORY
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE audit.login_history (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    username        VARCHAR(100),
    login_at        TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    logout_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    ip_address      INET,
    device_info     JSONB,
    status          VARCHAR(20),    -- success/failed/locked
    failure_reason  VARCHAR(200)
);
```
